## PRE-FLIGHT (execute ALL in parallel before reading anything)

```bash
git log --oneline -7                        # recent history + commit style
git status --short                          # uncommitted changes
git diff HEAD --stat                        # change scope
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | tail -20           # TypeScript strict check

docker ps --filter "name=mega-brain" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"

wc -l HANDOFF.md                            # total lines — drives the read-from-end offset
```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 125	## mcorch-qa-healing COMMIT_RE Pure-Decimal Tightening Record (2026-05-25)
> 126	
> 127	**QA AUDITOR TIGHTENING v6.8.3 SHIPPED (2026-05-25).** Sessão dedicada e curta para fechar definitivamente o false-positive da Fase 5c que forçou o workaround `87961ab` durante o seal v6.8.2. A regex `COMMIT_RE` em `scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts:18` matchava qualquer `[0-9a-f]{9,40}` entre crases, incluindo timestamps de migration puramente decimais (14 dígitos como `20260525173500`). O seal v6.8.2 contornou reescrevendo a citação no HANDOFF.md para o filename completo da migration; v6.8.3 fecha o bug na raiz: o loop `matchAll(COMMIT_RE)` ganhou guarda `if (/^\d+$/.test(hash)) continue;` que pula matches puramente decimais antes de chamar `push`. Comentário acima do regex estendido documenta a exclusão (referencia commits `87961ab` e `17ff2ca`). Mesma classe de input-validation tightening da FMEA-002 (`docs/bok/mcorch-qa-healing/`), que fechou um caso sibling onde COMMIT_RE matchava prefixos de 8-hex de UUID.
> 128	
> 129	**ORO triplet desta task (Lei 4):** Operator = MCORCH Master Execution Agent (eu); Reviewer = Sovereign (Gabriel Zarattini); Owner = Sovereign (Gabriel Zarattini — débito BoK opcional: skill `mcorch-qa-healing` já tem suite 9/9 selada, FMEA-002 cobre o pattern).
> 130	
> 131	**Cobertura preventiva.** Vitest case novo em `src/test/qa-extract-manifest.test.ts` — `"does not treat a pure-decimal migration timestamp as a commit"` — asserta que `` `20260525173500` `` NÃO registra como `git_commit` e `` `bfe2f43` `` ainda registra quando ambos aparecem no mesmo bloco markdown. Suite passou de 176 → 177 testes em 4.53s, sem regressão nos outros 9 arquivos.
> 132	
> 133	**Exemption do BoK Gate aplicada (CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol).** Single-file bug patch escopado a feature já documentada (skill `mcorch-qa-healing` com suite BoK 9/9 selada em v6.6.9). Nenhuma migration, nenhuma edge function tocada, nenhum schema mudou. Pure tooling fix — sem deploy.
> 134

> 136	|--------|--------|
> 137	| `scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts:97` — guarda pure-decimal no loop COMMIT_RE | ✅ `if (/^\d+$/.test(hash)) continue;` adicionada antes do `push` |
> 138	| `scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts:18-20` — comentário estendido | ✅ Documenta a exclusão referenciando regression v6.8.2 (commit `17ff2ca`) |
> 139	| `src/test/qa-extract-manifest.test.ts:107-115` — Vitest case novo | ✅ Cobre pure-decimal rejection + bfe2f43 still-matches no mesmo bloco |

> 141

> 147	### Arquitetura — Pure-decimal exclusion no extract-manifest
> 148	
> 149	```
> 150	COMMIT_RE = /`([0-9a-f]{7}|[0-9a-f]{9,40})`/gi
> 151	                              ↑
> 152	                              14-digit migration timestamps satisfy this branch
> 153	
> 154	for (const m of block.matchAll(COMMIT_RE)) {
> 155	  const hash = m[1].toLowerCase();
> 156	  if (/^\d+$/.test(hash)) continue;   ← v6.8.3 guarda (pure-decimal skip)
> 157	  push({ kind: "git_commit", expected: hash, ... });
> 158	}
> 159	```

Root cause materializado: migration `20260516224541_deduct_mco_coins_ledger.sql` introduziu uma versão 4-arg de `deduct_mco_coins` esperando que `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION` substituísse a 2-arg original. Como signatures eram diferentes, a função original foi mantida como **segundo overload**. PostgREST então rejeitava todas as chamadas `supabase-js .rpc()` passando 2 args com `PGRST203` ("Could not choose the best candidate function"), quebrando silenciosamente o `orchestrate-content` desde 2026-05-16. A regressão passou despercebida porque o último Run real bem-sucedido tinha sido em 2026-05-04 (21 dias antes desta sessão). Fix: nova migration `20260525173500_drop_deduct_mco_coins_overload.sql` (`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.deduct_mco_coins(uuid, decimal)`) deixa a 4-arg como única candidata; defaults `p_action='spend'` + `p_context='{}'` cobrem callers 2-arg sem mudanças em código. **OTD-LEDGER-OVERLOAD aberta e fechada na mesma sessão** (RPN ~200 calculada · severidade 8 · probabilidade 5 · detecção 5 → 0 pós-fix).

> 296	## Ollama × OpenClaw Provider Integration + Gateway Crash-Loop Recovery Record (2026-05-25)
> 297	
> 298	**OLLAMA × OPENCLAW v6.8.1 SHIPPED (2026-05-25).** Sessão de continuidade do trabalho iniciado em 2026-05-21 (interrompida por limite de contexto Sovereign; retomada após o Antigravity selar v6.8.0). Trabalho 100% em `~/.openclaw/` e `~/.claude/` (fora do repo) + selo aqui em `HANDOFF.md`. Cinco entregáveis materiais:
> 299	
> 300	**1. Cron mirror dos sonhos no painel OpenClaw.** Os 3 crons do ciclo circadiano (`nightly-stage1/2/3` @ 03/05/07 BRT no crontab do SO `ubuntu`) foram espelhados como **3 jobs disabled** em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` para visibilidade no painel "Tarefas Cron". Descoberta arquitetural confirmada via doc oficial OpenClaw: a página **Sonhos ≠ painel Cron** — Sonhos é exclusiva do plugin `memory-core` (1 sweep diário, fases light→REM→deep internas, não agendáveis individualmente).
> 301	
> 302	**2. Dreaming "0 promovido" — causa-raiz material, NÃO fixable do nosso lado.** Análise das 380 entradas do `short-term-recall.json` do agente `main`: `recallCount` é **0 em 380/380**, `maxScore` máximo **0.62** (gate ≥0.8), `uniqueQueries` máximo **1** (gate ≥3). **Os 3 gates de promoção são estruturalmente inalcançáveis** — não é "agentes pouco usados", é teto-de-métrica abaixo do gate. Interno ao `memory-core` do OpenClaw; refutada inclusive a hipótese inicial "Ollama causou" (Ollama não tem modelo de embedding instalado, embeddings 3072d são Gemini/OpenAI).
> 303	
> 304	**3. Ollama como provider OpenClaw — config wired.** Adicionado bloco `models.providers.ollama` em `~/.openclaw/openclaw.json` (`baseUrl http://localhost:11434`, `api:"ollama"`, `apiKey:"ollama-local"`). Agente `engineer` → `ollama/qwen3.5:latest` (9.7B · tools ✓). Demais 6 agentes (`main`, `artisan`, `scientist`, `marketing-growth`, `guardian`, `claw-master`) → `ollama/llama3.1:8b` (8B · tools ✓ · context 128K). Fallback `google/gemini-2.5-flash` em todos. Modelo `llama3.1:8b` puxado (4.9 GB). Limpeza: `llama3:latest` e `deepseek-coder:latest` removidos (ambos sem tools; o `llama3` dava 400 instantâneo, sustentado só pelo fallback).
> 305	
> 306	**4. INCIDENTE CRÍTICO — Gateway crash-loop de 716 restarts e RECOVERY.** Comando `openclaw gateway restart` para recarregar a config nova **desgarrou um processo órfão** (PPID 1) que ficou segurando a porta 18789. O PM2 (que de fato gere o Gateway como app **`maestro`**, id 0) ficou em crash-loop tentando subir sua própria instância → **716 restarts** no contador, log enchendo de `port 18789 already in use`. Recovery sequencial: `pm2 stop maestro` → `kill <órfão>` → `pm2 start maestro` → `pm2 save`. Estado final: 1 instância PM2-gerida, estável, restarts travados em 717.
> 307	
> 308	**5. LIÇÃO OPERACIONAL CRÍTICA (gravada na memória, fora do repo).** **NUNCA** usar `openclaw gateway restart` neste setup. **SEMPRE** usar **`pm2 restart maestro`** para recarregar config do Gateway. O serviço systemd `openclaw-gateway` está `disabled`/`failed` (ignorar — quem gere é o PM2). Lição registrada em `~/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/project_openclaw_ollama_integration.md` (auto-memory, persiste cross-conversation).
> 309	
> 310	**Teste E2E (Sovereign via Telegram).** Mensagem ao bot → agente `main`, modelo `llama3.1:8b`. Log: `provider=ollama model=llama3.1:8b` ✅ (integração CERTA, sem `does not support tools`), MAS `LLM request timed out` após ~4 min → **inferência CPU de 8B em 4-core ARM passa do timeout do OpenClaw**. Recomendação API-first emitida; o Sovereign aplicou manualmente via Telegram (entradas `google/gemini-3.5-flash`, `openrouter/auto`, `openrouter/qwen/qwen3.7-max` adicionadas em `agents.defaults.models`, primary mudado para `google/gemini-3.5-flash`) — visível nos logs auto-memória de sessão Telegram do dia (untracked, preservados em `stash@{0}: pre-handoff-mcorch-ollama-seal`).

> 369	**CANVAS STUDIO MAP & DIAGNOSTICS v6.8.0 SHIPPED (2026-05-25).** Sessão focada em (1) corrigir a falha de submissão de execuções no Canvas Studio mapeando as strings de `node_type` para valores válidos do enum Postgres `vm_node_type` de forma robusta e exaustiva, e (2) propagar o detalhamento dos erros Postgres na resposta HTTP 500 para permitir um diagnóstico mais rápido pelo Sovereign.

> 4332	### Embedding Model
> 4333	`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, `dimensions: 768` (truncated output via OpenRouter).

> 4335	
> 4336	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 4337	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 4338	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 4339	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 4340	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 4341	
> 4342	### Comandos de manutenção da Mesh
> 4343	```bash
> 4344	# Verificar nós sem embedding (deveria retornar array vazio):
> 4345	curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name" \
> 4346	  -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'{len(d)} nós sem embedding')"
> 4347	
> 4348	# Backfill manual (Bun-native — requer .env):
> 4349	bun run scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts
> 4350	
> 4351	# Backfill de código-fonte nos nós AST:
> 4352	bun run scripts/backfill-node-content.ts
> 4353	
> 4354	# Re-sync AST completo:
> 4355	cd packages/gitnexus-core && npm run sync-mesh
> 4356	
> 4357	# Acompanhar re-ingesta pós-commit:
> 4358	tail -f /tmp/mcorch-ingest.log
> 4359	```

> 4363	## Video Rescue Protocol
> 4364	
> 4365	When a video generation operation (Google Veo) times out or client disconnects:
> 4366	```bash
> 4367	# Via edge function (production):

> 4468	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. O motor Sigma.js/ForceAtlas2 apresentava loops de renderização infinitos ("blinking") e resets de estado ao interagir com a sidebar. A correção envolveu a estabilização de referências de props, memoização rigorosa das opções do grafo e desacoplamento de filtros dos ciclos de renderização do canvas. Paralelamente, a Matrix foi dotada de inteligência de código nativa via hook `useNodeIntelligence` e um `NodeDetailsPanel` reestruturado com navegação por abas.

> 9	
> 10	## 1. Scope Statement
> 11	
> 12	Este documento especifica os requisitos funcionais (FR) e não-funcionais (NFR) do `e2e-user-zero`. Deriva diretamente do PRD `03-prd.md` (12 PRs MoSCoW) e alimenta o SDD `05-sdd.md`.
> 13	
> 14	**In scope:**
> 15	- Runner CLI (`scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts`) — Bun-native, single-file entry, suporta 3 modes (dogfood/focused/regression) com flags estáveis.
> 16	- Browser driver layer (`scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/browser-driver.ts`) — wrapper sobre `agent-browser` CLI, abstrai chamadas para permitir fallback futuro.
> 17	- Flow definitions (`scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/flows/<name>.ts`) — declarative listing de comandos por jornada.
> 18	- 4-surface capture pipeline (DOM snapshot + HAR + console + screenshot).
> 19	- Findings classifier + persistor (observation node insert + OBSERVES edge).
> 20	- LGPD redaction layer (pre-persist transform).
> 21	- Phase 5c gate extension (`scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts` + `verifiers.ts`).
> 22	- Vitest test suite covering runner + driver + classifier + redactor.
> 23	- Default flows shipped: `trigger-orchestration`, `auth-login`, `dashboard-tour`, `canvas-overview` (excludes deep Canvas inspection — that's `audit-canvas-ui.ts`).
> 24	
> 25	**Out of scope:**
> 26	- Mobile viewport testing.
> 27	- Cross-browser (Firefox/Safari) — Chromium only.
> 28	- AI-powered finding triage (uses heuristics, not LLM, for v1).
> 29	- Multi-tenant E2E (deferred until Usuário 1+ is real).
> 30	- Cloud browser providers (Browserless/Browserbase) — local Chromium only.
> 31	- Visual pixel-diff regression — script logic only.
> 32	- Production synthetic monitoring (this is pre-merge gate, not always-on).
> 33	
> 34	---
> 35	
> 36	## 2. Functional Requirements
> 37	
> 38	> Category: `CLI` (runner) | `Driver` (browser-driver lib) | `Flow` (flow definitions) | `Classifier` (findings logic) | `Mesh` (observation persistence) | `Gate` (Phase 5c integration) | `Redactor` (LGPD)
> 39	
> 40	| ID | Title | Description | Priority | PR Traced | Category | Test ID |
> 41	|----|-------|-------------|----------|-----------|----------|---------|
> 42	| FR-E2E-001 | **Snapshot accessibility tree per checkpoint** | Runner deve invocar `agent-browser snapshot -i --json` em pontos de checkpoint (entrada de cada flow, antes/depois de cada interação significativa). Output salvo em `snapshots/<flow>-<step>.txt` para auditoria humana e em `findings.json[].snapshot_ref` para classifier | Critical | PR-E2E-001 | Driver | AT-E2E-001 |
> 43	| FR-E2E-002 | **HAR capture per flow** | Runner inicia `agent-browser network har start <path>` no início do flow e `network har stop` no fim. HAR resultante passa por LGPD redactor antes de persistir | Critical | PR-E2E-003 | Driver | AT-E2E-002 |
> 44	| FR-E2E-003 | **Console messages capture per flow** | Runner invoca `agent-browser get console-messages --json` ao final de cada flow (ou periódico se flow > 60s); persistido como JSONL em `console/<flow>.jsonl` | Critical | PR-E2E-003 | Driver | AT-E2E-003 |
> 45	| FR-E2E-004 | **Screenshot per finding** | Quando classifier identifica finding, runner toma screenshot anotada (`agent-browser screenshot --annotate <path>`) e referencia em `findings.json[].evidence_paths.screenshot` | Critical | PR-E2E-003 | Driver | AT-E2E-004 |
> 46	| FR-E2E-005 | **Three execution modes via --mode flag** | Runner aceita `--mode dogfood\|focused\|regression`. Cada modo tem entry function dedicada em runner.ts. Modo default = focused (mais comum em Phase 5c). | Critical | PR-E2E-001, PR-E2E-002 | CLI | AT-E2E-005 |
> 47	| FR-E2E-006 | **Flow definition contract** | Cada arquivo em `flows/<name>.ts` exporta `default async function (driver, ctx) => Promise<FlowResult>`. Driver é instância de BrowserDriver; ctx contém run_id, output_dir, auth_state_path. Flow nunca chama `agent-browser` direto — sempre via driver | Critical | PR-E2E-002 | Flow | AT-E2E-006 |
> 48	| FR-E2E-007 | **Findings classifier + observation node insert** | Após cada flow, classifier analisa: (a) HAR para HTTP 4xx/5xx sem retry; (b) console para `console.error`/`unhandledrejection`; (c) snapshot para missing elements esperados; (d) screenshot dimensões para black/blank canvas regression. Cada finding gera 1 row em `mcorch_nodes` (service role insert) + 1 edge `OBSERVES` para módulo afetado quando identificável | Critical | PR-E2E-005 | Classifier + Mesh | AT-E2E-007 |
> 49	| FR-E2E-008 | **LGPD redaction transform pre-persist** | Antes de salvar HAR, runner aplica transformações: headers `Authorization`, `Cookie`, `apikey`, `x-api-key`, `x-supabase-*` → `<redacted>`; bodies em rotas listadas em `e2e-config.json:redactedPaths` → `<body-redacted>`. Screenshots em rotas autenticadas pixelize regiões com classe CSS `.sensitive-data` (best-effort) | Critical | PR-E2E-006 | Redactor | AT-E2E-008 |
> 50	| FR-E2E-009 | **Auth state lifecycle handling** | `--state <path>` injetado via driver inicial. Se HAR detecta HTTP 401 com `WWW-Authenticate` em path autenticado, runner aborta com exit code 3 + mensagem PT-BR + insere `infra_health_logs` event_type `e2e_user_zero_auth_expired` | Critical | PR-E2E-007 | Driver + CLI | AT-E2E-009 |
> 51	| FR-E2E-010 | **Phase 5c manifest emission** | Quando claim `e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <run_id>` aparece no HANDOFF.md, `scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts` emite ClaimKind `e2e_user_zero`. `scripts/qa/verifiers.ts:verifyE2EUserZero()` valida: (a) `e2e-output/run-<id>/report.md` existe; (b) report contém badge `✅ GO`; (c) `findings.json` parses + summary.p0_count = 0 + summary.p1_count = 0; (d) `commands.jsonl` first timestamp dentro de 7 dias do seal | Critical | PR-E2E-008 | Gate | AT-E2E-010 |
> 52	| FR-E2E-011 | **excludedPaths config** | `e2e-config.json` (committed defaults) tem `excludedPaths: ['/dashboard/canvas/*']`. Dogfood mode pula flows que tocam esses paths. Override CLI `--exclude-paths '<json-array>'` permitido para experimentação | High | PR-E2E-009 | CLI + Flow | AT-E2E-011 |
> 53	| FR-E2E-012 | **Regression mode + diff** | `--mode regression --baseline <run_id>` carrega `e2e-output/run-<baseline>/commands.jsonl`, replay via driver, gera `regression-diff.md` listando: findings novos (vermelho), findings resolvidos (verde), findings persistentes (amarelo). Exit code 1 se houver finding novo P0/P1 | High | PR-E2E-010 | CLI + Classifier | AT-E2E-012 |
> 54	| FR-E2E-013 | **Infra failure telemetry** | Runner que falha por motivo infra (não finding) emite `infra_health_logs` event com payload `{ run_id, mode, failure_phase, error_message, stack? }`. Failure phases enumerados: `browser_launch_timeout`, `auth_state_corrupt`, `har_write_failed`, `mesh_insert_failed`, `output_dir_create_failed` | High | PR-E2E-011 | CLI | AT-E2E-013 |
> 55	| FR-E2E-014 | **Zero mcoCoins consumption** | Nenhum codepath do runner invoca `supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins')`. Service role usado apenas para `mcorch_nodes.insert` (observation) e `infra_health_logs.insert` (telemetry). Verificado por test que faz `grep -E 'deduct_mco_coins' scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/**/*.ts` retorna zero | High | PR-E2E-012 | CLI + Mesh | AT-E2E-014 |
> 56	| FR-E2E-015 | **Idempotent runs (safe re-execution)** | Re-executar runner com mesmo `--out` directory NÃO duplica observation nodes. Idempotency key = sha256(flow_name + finding.title + finding.evidence_paths.screenshot) → dedup pre-insert. Same key → UPDATE not INSERT | High | PR-E2E-004 | Mesh | AT-E2E-015 |
> 57	| FR-E2E-016 | **JSON output mode** | `--json` flag suprime markdown stdout, emite apenas final summary JSON em stdout. Útil para skill bok-scribe ou outras consumers parsing programático | Medium | PR-E2E-004 | CLI | AT-E2E-016 |
> 58	| FR-E2E-017 | **Help message** | `--help` ou `-h` printa usage + flag descriptions + 3 exemplos comuns. Não invoca browser | Medium | PR-E2E-004 | CLI | AT-E2E-017 |
> 59	| FR-E2E-018 | **Output directory auto-creation + .gitignore seed** | Primeira execução cria `e2e-output/` + adiciona linha `e2e-output/` em `.gitignore` se ausente. Não falha se já existir | Medium | PR-E2E-004 | CLI | AT-E2E-018 |
> 60	| FR-E2E-019 | **Findings JSON schema** | `findings.json` segue schema versionado em `scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/finding-schema.ts`. Mudanças breaking exigem version bump (`schema_version: "1.0.0"` no top). Permite consumers terceiros parsing seguro | Medium | PR-E2E-005 | Classifier | AT-E2E-019 |
> 61	| FR-E2E-020 | **Commands log for replay** | Runner persiste `commands.jsonl` com cada `agent-browser` call (timestamp + command + args + stdout_brief) para regression mode replay. Sensitive args (auth state path) NÃO persistidos | Medium | PR-E2E-010 | Driver | AT-E2E-020 |
> 62	
> 63	---
> 64	
> 65	## 3. Non-Functional Requirements
> 66	
> 67	| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |
> 68	|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|
> 69	| NFR-E2E-001 | Performance | Focused mode wall-clock time | p50 < 60s, p95 < 90s | Stopwatch em runner.ts, persistido em `infra_health_logs` |
> 70	| NFR-E2E-002 | Performance | Dogfood mode wall-clock time | p50 < 4 min, p95 < 6 min | Idem |
> 71	| NFR-E2E-003 | Performance | HAR file size per flow | p95 < 2 MB | `du -h har/<flow>.har` post-redaction |
> 72	| NFR-E2E-004 | Performance | Total run output dir size | p95 < 50 MB per run | `du -sh e2e-output/run-<id>/` |
> 73	| NFR-E2E-005 | Scalability | Concurrent runs (with --session per run) | Up to 4 concurrent sem cross-contamination | Manual stress test |
> 74	| NFR-E2E-006 | Reliability | Run completion rate (no infra failure) | ≥ 95% | `% runs com exit code ∈ {0,1} / total runs` em `infra_health_logs` |
> 75	| NFR-E2E-007 | Maintainability | Cyclomatic complexity per function in runner.ts/driver.ts | ≤ 10 | ESLint complexity rule |
> 76	| NFR-E2E-008 | Security | Zero plain-text secrets em committed artifacts | 100% | Pre-commit hook regex (BAC-E2E-005) |
> 77	| NFR-E2E-009 | Security | Auth state file permissions | 0600 (owner read/write only) | `stat -c %a <file>` check em test |
> 78	| NFR-E2E-010 | Compatibility | Bun version supported | ≥ 1.3.0 (current host: 1.3.12) | `bun --version` precondition no runner |
> 79	| NFR-E2E-011 | Compatibility | Node.js version supported | ≥ 22.x (current host: v22.22.3) | `node --version` (only se Bun não disponível em fallback futuro; v1 só Bun) |
> 80	| NFR-E2E-012 | Compatibility | agent-browser CLI version | ≥ 0.27.0 (pinned baseline) | `agent-browser --version` precondition |
> 81	| NFR-E2E-013 | Observability | Each run emits at minimum 1 INFO log para `infra_health_logs` (success ou failure) | 100% | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM infra_health_logs WHERE service='e2e-user-zero'` vs run count |
> 82	| NFR-E2E-014 | Privacy (LGPD) | Auth state files são gitignored | 100% | `git check-ignore ~/.claude/auth-states/*.json` passa |
> 83	| NFR-E2E-015 | Privacy (LGPD) | HAR redaction false-negative rate (secrets que passam) | 0 per LGPD compliance review (quarterly) | Manual audit dos últimos 10 HARs |
> 84	| NFR-E2E-016 | Resilience | Recover from Chrome zombie process | `agent-browser close --all` em finally block em runner.ts | Code review |
> 85	| NFR-E2E-017 | Resilience | Recover from filesystem ENOSPC (disk full) | Runner detecta + exit code 2 + telemetry event `disk_full` | Manual test (fill disk to <100MB free) |
> 86	
> 87	---
> 88	
> 89	## 4. Interface Requirements
> 90	
> 91	### 4.1 CLI Contract
> 92	
> 93	```bash
> 94	# Synopsis
> 95	bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts [options]
> 96	
> 97	# Options:
> 98	  --mode <dogfood|focused|regression>      Default: focused
> 99	  --flow <name>                            Required if --mode focused; ignored otherwise
> 100	  --baseline <run_id>                      Required if --mode regression
> 101	  --state <path>                           Path to auth state JSON; default: none (unauthenticated)
> 102	  --out <dir>                              Output dir override; default: ./e2e-output/run-<auto-ts>/
> 103	  --video                                  Enable video recording (OTD-E2E-005)
> 104	  --headed                                 Run with visible browser window (debug)
> 105	  --exclude-paths <json-array>             Override default excludedPaths
> 106	  --json                                   Emit only final summary JSON to stdout
> 107	  --help, -h                               Show usage and exit
> 108	
> 109	# Exit codes:
> 110	  0    Run complete, zero P0/P1 findings (GO)
> 111	  1    Run complete, P0 or P1 findings present (NO-GO)
> 112	  2    Infra failure (browser, fs, network) — telemetry emitted
> 113	  3    Auth state expired or missing — refresh required
> 114	  4    Invalid arguments or missing required flag
> 115	```
> 116	
> 117	### 4.2 Internal Interface — runner ↔ BrowserDriver
> 118	
> 119	```typescript
> 120	// scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/browser-driver.ts
> 121	
> 122	export interface BrowserDriver {
> 123	  open(url: string): Promise<void>
> 124	  snapshot(opts?: SnapshotOpts): Promise<SnapshotResult>      // returns refs + a11y tree
> 125	  click(refOrSelector: string): Promise<void>
> 126	  fill(refOrSelector: string, text: string): Promise<void>
> 127	  press(key: string): Promise<void>
> 128	  wait(opts: { ref?: string; ms?: number; load?: 'networkidle' | 'domcontent' }): Promise<void>
> 129	  screenshot(path: string, opts?: { annotate?: boolean }): Promise<void>
> 130	  startHAR(path: string): Promise<void>
> 131	  stopHAR(): Promise<void>
> 132	  getConsoleMessages(opts?: { since?: number }): Promise<ConsoleMessage[]>
> 133	  getNetworkRequests(opts?: { filter?: string }): Promise<NetworkRequest[]>
> 134	  close(opts?: { all?: boolean }): Promise<void>
> 135	  // Idempotent: calling twice with same args is safe
> 136	}
> 137	
> 138	export interface SnapshotResult {
> 139	  page: { title: string; url: string }
> 140	  tree: AccessibilityNode[]   // refs included as @e1, @e2, ...
> 141	  text: string                // human-readable representation
> 142	  raw_command_output: string  // for debugging
> 143	}
> 144	
> 145	export interface ConsoleMessage {
> 146	  type: 'log' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'debug'
> 147	  text: string
> 148	  source?: { url: string; line: number; column: number }
> 149	  timestamp: number
> 150	}
> 151	```
> 152	
> 153	### 4.3 Internal Interface — runner ↔ Classifier
> 154	
> 155	```typescript
> 156	// scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/classifier.ts
> 157	
> 158	export interface Finding {
> 159	  id: string                                                            // F-001, F-002...
> 160	  flow: string                                                          // flow name
> 161	  severity: 'P0' | 'P1' | 'P2' | 'P3'
> 162	  issue_type: 'bug' | 'ux' | 'perf' | 'accessibility' | 'security' | 'regression'
> 163	  title: string
> 164	  description: string
> 165	  repro_steps: string[]                                                 // command-by-command
> 166	  evidence_paths: {
> 167	    screenshot?: string
> 168	    har_snippet?: string
> 169	    console_snippet?: string
> 170	    snapshot_ref?: string
> 171	  }
> 172	  affected_module?: { node_id?: string; node_name?: string; path?: string }
> 173	  suggested_fix?: string
> 174	  detected_at: string                                                   // ISO 8601
> 175	}
> 176	
> 177	export interface ClassifyInput {
> 178	  har: HARFile
> 179	  console: ConsoleMessage[]
> 180	  snapshots: SnapshotResult[]
> 181	  screenshots: { path: string; ts: number }[]
> 182	  flow: string
> 183	  ctx: RunContext
> 184	}
> 185	
> 186	export function classify(input: ClassifyInput): Finding[]               // synchronous, deterministic
> 187	```
> 188	
> 189	### 4.4 External Interface — Knowledge Mesh Insert
> 190	
> 191	```typescript
> 192	// Insert per finding
> 193	const { data: node } = await supabaseServiceRole
> 194	  .from('mcorch_nodes')
> 195	  .insert({
> 196	    node_type: 'observation',
> 197	    name: `e2e-finding-${finding.id}-${runId}`,
> 198	    content: `[${finding.severity}/${finding.issue_type}] ${finding.title}\n\n${finding.description}`,
> 199	    user_id: SOVEREIGN_USER_ID,  // hardcoded para v1 (single-tenant)
> 200	    project_id: null,
> 201	    metadata: {
> 202	      source: 'e2e-user-zero',
> 203	      run_id: runId,
> 204	      flow: finding.flow,
> 205	      severity: finding.severity,
> 206	      issue_type: finding.issue_type,
> 207	      evidence_path: `e2e-output/run-${runId}/`,
> 208	      schema_version: '1.0.0'
> 209	    },
> 210	    stability_score: severity === 'P0' ? 0.9 : severity === 'P1' ? 0.8 : 0.6
> 211	  })
> 212	  .select('id')
> 213	  .single()
> 214	
> 215	// Insert OBSERVES edge if affected_module identified
> 216	if (finding.affected_module?.node_id) {
> 217	  await supabaseServiceRole
> 218	    .from('mcorch_edges')
> 219	    .insert({
> 220	      source_id: node.id,
> 221	      target_id: finding.affected_module.node_id,
> 222	      relation_type: 'OBSERVES',
> 223	      weight: severity === 'P0' ? 1.0 : severity === 'P1' ? 0.7 : 0.4,
> 224	      metadata: { run_id: runId, flow: finding.flow, source: 'e2e-user-zero' }
> 225	    })
> 226	}
> 227	```
> 228	
> 229	### 4.5 Phase 5c Gate Contract Extension
> 230	
> 231	```typescript
> 232	// scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts (extension)
> 233	
> 234	// New ClaimKind
> 235	type ClaimKind =
> 236	  | 'git_commit'
> 237	  | 'supabase_uuid'
> 238	  | 'test_count'
> 239	  | 'sql_select_returning'
> 240	  | 'oro_compliance'
> 241	  | 'e2e_user_zero'              // NEW
> 242	
> 243	// Extracted from HANDOFF.md block:
> 244	// `e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <run_id>` ou
> 245	// `**e2e_user_zero_last_pass:** \`<run_id>\``
> 246	const E2E_RE = /(?:^|\n)\s*(?:\*\*)?e2e_user_zero_last_pass(?:\*\*)?\s*:\s*`?([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)`?/g
> 247	
> 248	// scripts/qa/verifiers.ts (extension)
> 249	export async function verifyE2EUserZero(claim: Claim): Promise<VerifyResult> {
> 250	  const runId = claim.expected
> 251	  const runDir = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'e2e-output', `run-${runId}`)
> 252	
> 253	  // Gate 1: directory exists
> 254	  if (!existsSync(runDir)) {
> 255	    return { status: 'failed', reason: `Run directory not found: ${runDir}` }
> 256	  }
> 257	
> 258	  // Gate 2: report.md exists and shows ✅ GO
> 259	  const report = readFileSync(path.join(runDir, 'report.md'), 'utf-8')
> 260	  if (!report.includes('✅ GO')) {
> 261	    return { status: 'failed', reason: 'Report does not show GO badge' }
> 262	  }
> 263	
> 264	  // Gate 3: findings.json shows zero P0/P1
> 265	  const findings = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(runDir, 'findings.json'), 'utf-8'))
> 266	  if ((findings.summary.p0_count ?? 0) > 0 || (findings.summary.p1_count ?? 0) > 0) {
> 267	    return { status: 'failed', reason: `Findings present: P0=${findings.summary.p0_count}, P1=${findings.summary.p1_count}` }
> 268	  }
> 269	
> 270	  // Gate 4: run timestamp within 7 days
> 271	  const runTs = new Date(findings.run_started_at).getTime()
> 272	  const ageHours = (Date.now() - runTs) / 36e5
> 273	  if (ageHours > 168) {
> 274	    return { status: 'failed', reason: `Run is ${Math.floor(ageHours / 24)} days old (max 7)` }
> 275	  }
> 276	
> 277	  return { status: 'verified' }
> 278	}
> 279	```
> 280

> 283	## 5. Data Requirements
> 284	
> 285	| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |
> 286	|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|
> 287	| `--mode` | enum | No (default `focused`) | ∈ {`dogfood`, `focused`, `regression`} | "Modo inválido — use dogfood, focused ou regression" |
> 288	| `--flow` | string | Yes (required if mode=focused) | Existe como `flows/<name>.ts` | "Flow não encontrado: flows/<name>.ts — flows disponíveis: …" |
> 289	| `--baseline` | string | Yes (required if mode=regression) | Existe como `e2e-output/run-<baseline>/commands.jsonl` | "Baseline run não encontrado em e2e-output/" |
> 290	| `--state` | path | Yes | File exists + perm 0600 + JSON parses | "Auth state inválida: <reason>" |
> 291	| `--out` | path | Yes (auto) | Parent dir existe; runner cria child | "Diretório pai inexistente: <path>" |
> 292	| HANDOFF.md `e2e_user_zero_last_pass` | string | Yes | Matches `^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$` (run_id slug) | "Run ID malformado: <value>" |
> 293	| Finding `severity` | enum | No | ∈ {`P0`, `P1`, `P2`, `P3`} | (não user-facing — internal contract) |
> 294	| Finding `issue_type` | enum | No | ∈ {`bug`, `ux`, `perf`, `accessibility`, `security`, `regression`} | (idem) |
> 295	| `infra_health_logs.payload.failure_phase` | enum | Yes (apenas em failure events) | ∈ enum listed in FR-E2E-013 | (idem) |
> 296	| Observation node `metadata.source` | string | No | === `e2e-user-zero` (literal) | (idem) |

> 10	## 1. Architecture Overview
> 11	
> 12	`e2e-user-zero` é **tooling interno CLI-first**, sem UI dedicada. Difere da Harness Engineering canônica do MCORCH (component → hook → edge function → DB) porque não há frontend nem hook React. O design preserva o espírito da Harness — separação clara de camadas — adaptado para Bun script:
> 13	
> 14	```
> 15	┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
> 16	│  CLI LAYER                                                    │
> 17	│  scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts                          │
> 18	│  · arg parse · mode dispatch · output dir mgmt · exit codes  │
> 19	└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
> 20	                       │ calls
> 21	┌──────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────────────┐
> 22	│  FLOW LAYER (declarative jornadas)                            │
> 23	│  scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/flows/<name>.ts                    │
> 24	│  · trigger-orchestration · auth-login · dashboard-tour · ... │
> 25	└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
> 26	                       │ uses
> 27	┌──────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────────────┐
> 28	│  DRIVER LAYER (abstração sobre agent-browser CLI)             │
> 29	│  scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/browser-driver.ts              │
> 30	│  · open · snapshot · click · fill · HAR · console · screenshot│
> 31	└──────┬──────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
> 32	       │ spawns                   │
> 33	┌──────▼────────────┐  ┌──────────▼─────────────────────────────┐
> 34	│  agent-browser    │  │  CAPTURE LAYER                          │
> 35	│  CLI v0.27.0+     │  │  · LGPD redactor (HAR + screenshots)    │
> 36	│  (subprocess)     │  │  · classifier (heuristic, deterministic)│
> 37	│  Chromium headless│  │  · finding schema validation            │
> 38	└───────────────────┘  └──────────────┬──────────────────────────┘
> 39	                                      │ persists via
> 40	                       ┌──────────────▼─────────────────────────┐
> 41	                       │  PERSISTENCE LAYER                      │
> 42	                       │  · Filesystem (e2e-output/)             │
> 43	                       │  · Supabase service role:               │
> 44	                       │    - mcorch_nodes (observation)         │
> 45	                       │    - mcorch_edges (OBSERVES)            │
> 46	                       │    - infra_health_logs (telemetry)      │
> 47	                       └─────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 48	
> 49	                       ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
> 50	                       │  GATE INTEGRATION (Phase 5c)            │
> 51	                       │  · scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts       │
> 52	                       │    + new ClaimKind 'e2e_user_zero'      │
> 53	                       │  · scripts/qa/verifiers.ts              │
> 54	                       │    + new verifyE2EUserZero()            │
> 55	                       └─────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 56	```
> 57	
> 58	**Architectural principles preserved from Harness Engineering:**
> 59	- **Single responsibility per layer**: runner only orchestrates; driver only translates; flows only define sequences; classifier only judges.
> 60	- **Pure functions where possible**: classifier is deterministic (same input → same findings).
> 61	- **Side effects gated by contract**: only persistence layer touches FS + DB.
> 62

> 108	### 2.3 New Edge Functions (`supabase/functions/`)
> 109	
> 110	**None.** Este BoK não introduz Edge Function nova. Toda persistência usa service role direto (via `@supabase/supabase-js` em script Bun). Justificativa:
> 111	
> 112	1. Runner é tooling interno, não user-facing — não há fluxo HTTP usuário → função.
> 113	2. Service role já está disponível ao agent (vault `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`) via `.env`.
> 114	3. Adding Edge Function adicionaria latência rede + deploy overhead sem benefício.
> 115	4. Mesh-side RLS continua intacta — observations são `user_id=Sovereign` literal (single-tenant até Usuário 1+).
> 116

> 118	
> 119	**None.** Sem UI, sem state global frontend.

> 123	**None.** Zero new migrations.

> 126	- `mcorch_nodes` — insert `node_type='observation'` (já existe; auto-embed via `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` para enriquecer semântica).
> 127	- `mcorch_edges` — insert `relationship='OBSERVES'` (relationship column é text livre; já há precedentes `DEFINES`, `CALLS`, `INHABITS`, `DERIVES_FROM`, `observes`, `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO`, `CONTAINS_LINK_FOR`).
> 128	- `infra_health_logs` — insert `service='e2e-user-zero'` (idem text livre).

> 132	## 3. Data Flow Diagrams
> 133	
> 134	### Use Case 1: Focused mode run (Phase 5c trigger)
> 135	
> 136	```
> 137	Agent invokes /handoff
> 138	  │
> 139	  ├──[Phase 5b: extract-manifest]──▶ detects file change in src/components/
> 140	  ├──[Phase 5c entry]──▶ runs mcorch-qa-healing engine
> 141	  │                          │
> 142	  │                          └──[verify e2e_user_zero claim]──▶ MISSING
> 143	  │                                                              │
> 144	  └──[runner invoked]                                           SEAL BLOCKED → agent must run E2E
> 145	  │
> 146	  ▼
> 147	runner.ts --mode focused --flow trigger-orchestration --state ~/.claude/auth-states/login-mcorch-prod.json
> 148	  │
> 149	  ├──[arg parse + validate]──▶ exit 4 if invalid
> 150	  ├──[output dir create]──▶ e2e-output/run-2026-05-25-19-30-a1b2/
> 151	  ├──[load flow]──▶ flows/trigger-orchestration.ts
> 152	  ├──[driver.spawn agent-browser --session focused-2026-05-25-19-30 ...]
> 153	  │     │
> 154	  │     ├──[network har start]──▶ har/trigger-orchestration.har
> 155	  │     ├──[open login.mcorch.com/dashboard/orchestration]
> 156	  │     ├──[snapshot -i]──▶ snapshots/trigger-orchestration-01.txt + refs
> 157	  │     ├──[click @e<Trigger button ref>]
> 158	  │     ├──[fill @e<topic input ref> "test topic"]
> 159	  │     ├──[click @e<Run button ref>]
> 160	  │     ├──[wait --load networkidle + wait 3000]
> 161	  │     ├──[snapshot -i]──▶ snapshots/trigger-orchestration-02.txt
> 162	  │     ├──[get console-messages]──▶ console/trigger-orchestration.jsonl
> 163	  │     ├──[screenshot]──▶ screenshots/trigger-orchestration-final.png
> 164	  │     └──[network har stop + close]
> 165	  │
> 166	  ├──[redactor.transformHAR(har/trigger-orchestration.har)]──▶ writes redacted in-place
> 167	  ├──[classifier.classify({ har, console, snapshots, screenshots })]──▶ Finding[]
> 168	  │     │
> 169	  │     └──[heuristics]:
> 170	  │           - HTTP 4xx/5xx without retry → bug P0/P1
> 171	  │           - console.error count > 0 → bug P1/P2
> 172	  │           - missing expected ref (e.g., pipeline_run UUID in snapshot) → regression P0
> 173	  │           - LCP > 2.5s detected via network timing → perf P3
> 174	  │
> 175	  ├──[mesh-persistor.insertFindings(findings)]──▶ service-role inserts
> 176	  │     │
> 177	  │     ├──[for each finding] INSERT INTO mcorch_nodes (node_type='observation', ...)
> 178	  │     ├──[if affected_module identified] INSERT INTO mcorch_edges (relationship='OBSERVES', ...)
> 179	  │     └──[trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert fires]──▶ pg_net call to embed-mcorch-node (async)
> 180	  │
> 181	  ├──[report-renderer.render(findings)]──▶ report.md + findings.json
> 182	  ├──[telemetry.emitRunComplete(run_id, summary)]──▶ infra_health_logs row
> 183	  │
> 184	  └──[exit code based on findings.summary.p0_count + p1_count]
> 185	         │
> 186	         └─▶ Agent reads report.md, includes `e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <run_id>` in HANDOFF.md
> 187	                │
> 188	                └─▶ Re-run Phase 5c → verifyE2EUserZero() → VERIFIED → SEAL ALLOWED
> 189	```
> 190	
> 191	### Use Case 2: Regression replay
> 192	
> 193	```
> 194	Agent suspects hotfix verification
> 195	  │
> 196	  ▼
> 197	runner.ts --mode regression --baseline run-2026-05-20-14-00-xyz
> 198	  │
> 199	  ├──[load baseline commands.jsonl]──▶ array of {command, args, expected_response_brief}
> 200	  ├──[driver.startSession]
> 201	  ├──[for each command in baseline]
> 202	  │     ├──[driver.execute(command, args)]
> 203	  │     ├──[snapshot at same checkpoints as baseline]
> 204	  │     ├──[capture HAR + console + screenshots]
> 205	  │     └──[record actual vs expected]
> 206	  │
> 207	  ├──[classifier.classify(current)]──▶ currentFindings
> 208	  ├──[load baseline findings.json]──▶ baselineFindings
> 209	  ├──[regression-diff.diff(baselineFindings, currentFindings)]──▶ diff object
> 210	  │     │
> 211	  │     ├──[resolved: in baseline, not in current] (verde)
> 212	  │     ├──[persistent: in both] (amarelo)
> 213	  │     └──[new: not in baseline, in current] (vermelho — critical signal)
> 214	  │
> 215	  ├──[render regression-diff.md + report.md]
> 216	  ├──[exit code 1 if any new P0/P1; 0 otherwise]
> 217	```

> 237	
> 238	## 4. API Contract Definitions
> 239	
> 240	### 4.1 Internal: Driver ↔ agent-browser CLI
> 241	
> 242	> Driver translates declarative method calls into `Bun.spawn(['agent-browser', ...args])`. No HTTP, no JSON-RPC — process spawn + stdout parsing.
> 243	
> 244	```typescript
> 245	// scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/browser-driver.ts (excerpt)
> 246	
> 247	import { spawn } from 'bun'
> 248	
> 249	export class BrowserDriverImpl implements BrowserDriver {
> 250	  private sessionName: string
> 251	  private commandsLog: WritableStream
> 252	
> 253	  async snapshot(opts?: { interactive?: boolean }): Promise<SnapshotResult> {
> 254	    const args = ['--session', this.sessionName, 'snapshot']
> 255	    if (opts?.interactive !== false) args.push('-i')
> 256	    args.push('--json')
> 257	
> 258	    const proc = spawn(['agent-browser', ...args], { stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' })
> 259	    const out = await new Response(proc.stdout).text()
> 260	    const exit = await proc.exited
> 261	
> 262	    if (exit !== 0) {
> 263	      const err = await new Response(proc.stderr).text()
> 264	      throw new BrowserDriverError(`snapshot failed: ${err}`)
> 265	    }
> 266	
> 267	    this.logCommand('snapshot', args, out.slice(0, 200))
> 268	    return JSON.parse(out)
> 269	  }
> 270	
> 271	  // ... outras methods ...
> 272	
> 273	  async close(opts?: { all?: boolean }): Promise<void> {
> 274	    const args = ['close']
> 275	    if (opts?.all) args.push('--all')
> 276	    else args.unshift('--session', this.sessionName)
> 277	    await spawn(['agent-browser', ...args]).exited
> 278	  }
> 279	}
> 280	```
> 281	
> 282	### 4.2 Internal: Mesh Persistor ↔ Supabase
> 283	
> 284	```typescript
> 285	// scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/mesh-persistor.ts (excerpt)
> 286	
> 287	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
> 288	
> 289	const serviceRole = createClient(
> 290	  Bun.env.SUPABASE_URL!,
> 291	  Bun.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
> 292	  { auth: { persistSession: false } }
> 293	)
> 294	
> 295	const SOVEREIGN_USER_ID = Bun.env.SOVEREIGN_USER_ID  // exposed via .env (NOT a secret — it's a UUID)
> 296	
> 297	export async function insertFindings(
> 298	  findings: Finding[],
> 299	  runId: string
> 300	): Promise<{ inserted: number; deduped: number }> {
> 301	  let inserted = 0
> 302	  let deduped = 0
> 303	
> 304	  for (const finding of findings) {
> 305	    const idempKey = await sha256(`${finding.flow}|${finding.title}|${finding.evidence_paths.screenshot ?? ''}`)
> 306	    const nodeName = `e2e-finding-${idempKey.slice(0, 12)}`
> 307	
> 308	    // Dedup check
> 309	    const { data: existing } = await serviceRole
> 310	      .from('mcorch_nodes')
> 311	      .select('id')
> 312	      .eq('name', nodeName)
> 313	      .maybeSingle()
> 314	
> 315	    if (existing) {
> 316	      deduped++
> 317	      continue
> 318	    }
> 319	
> 320	    const { data: node, error } = await serviceRole
> 321	      .from('mcorch_nodes')
> 322	      .insert({
> 323	        node_type: 'observation',
> 324	        name: nodeName,
> 325	        content: renderFindingContent(finding),
> 326	        user_id: SOVEREIGN_USER_ID,
> 327	        metadata: {
> 328	          source: 'e2e-user-zero',
> 329	          run_id: runId,
> 330	          flow: finding.flow,
> 331	          severity: finding.severity,
> 332	          issue_type: finding.issue_type,
> 333	          evidence_path: `e2e-output/run-${runId}/`,
> 334	          idempotency_key: idempKey,
> 335	          schema_version: '1.0.0'
> 336	        },
> 337	        stability_score: severityToStability(finding.severity)
> 338	      })
> 339	      .select('id')
> 340	      .single()
> 341	
> 342	    if (error) {
> 343	      console.error(`mesh insert failed for finding ${finding.id}:`, error.message)
> 344	      continue
> 345	    }
> 346	
> 347	    if (finding.affected_module?.node_id) {
> 348	      await serviceRole
> 349	        .from('mcorch_edges')
> 350	        .insert({
> 351	          source_id: node.id,
> 352	          target_id: finding.affected_module.node_id,
> 353	          relation_type: 'OBSERVES',
> 354	          weight: severityToWeight(finding.severity),
> 355	          metadata: { run_id: runId, flow: finding.flow, source: 'e2e-user-zero' }
> 356	        })
> 357	    }
> 358	
> 359	    inserted++
> 360	  }
> 361	
> 362	  return { inserted, deduped }
> 363	}
> 364	```
> 365	
> 366	### 4.3 External: Phase 5c manifest emission contract
> 367	
> 368	```markdown
> 369	<!-- Em HANDOFF.md, dentro do bloco da seal: -->
> 370	
> 371	**Material proofs:**
> 372	- Commit: `<hash>`
> 373	- ...
> 374	- **e2e_user_zero_last_pass:** `run-2026-05-25-19-30-a1b2`   ← claim emitido pelo agent quando aplicável
> 375	```
> 376	
> 377	`extract-manifest.ts` parseia tanto a forma bullet com **bold** quanto a inline `e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <id>`.
> 378	
> 379	### 4.4 No Edge Function — sem contrato HTTP/JWT externo
> 380	
> 381	Nenhuma rota Supabase Functions é adicionada. Toda comunicação Mesh ↔ runner é via service role direta no script.
> 382	
> 383	---
> 384	
> 385	## 5. Security Design
> 386	
> 387	### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)
> 388	
> 389	| Component | Threat | STRIDE Type | Severity | Likelihood | Mitigation |
> 390	|-----------|--------|-------------|----------|------------|------------|
> 391	| `runner.ts` arg parser | Injection via `--flow` reading unintended file (`../../../etc/passwd`) | Tampering | Medium | Low | Whitelist: `--flow <name>` must match `flows/<name>.ts` resolved path; reject if resolved path escapes `flows/` |
> 392	| `BrowserDriverImpl.snapshot/click/etc.` | Command injection via maliciously crafted ref/selector argument | Tampering | High | Low | Driver uses argv array (not shell string); `Bun.spawn(['agent-browser', ...args])` — no shell interpolation |
> 393	| HAR file post-capture | Token leakage (JWT, API keys, Stripe secrets) in committed artifact | Information Disclosure | **Critical** | Medium (default behavior captures everything) | (a) Redactor transform pre-persist; (b) `.gitignore` `e2e-output/`; (c) Pre-commit hook regex scan; (d) NFR-E2E-015 quarterly audit |
> 394	| Auth state file | Theft of Sovereign session token | Information Disclosure | **Critical** | Low (filesystem perm) | (a) `chmod 600` enforced on write; (b) `~/.claude/auth-states/` outside repo; (c) 30-day rotation policy; (d) Detect HTTP 401 → exit 3 |
> 395	| Screenshots | PII captured (mco_balance, content_library titles, user email) | Information Disclosure | High | Medium | (a) `.gitignore` already covers; (b) For multi-tenant future: pixelize sensitive regions via CSS class detection |
> 396	| `mesh-persistor.ts` service role | Service role key leaked via process listing or logs | Information Disclosure | **Critical** | Low | (a) Read from env (`Bun.env`); (b) Never logged; (c) Never written to artifacts (commands.jsonl strips); (d) Vault rotation policy upstream |
> 397	| `mcorch_edges` OBSERVES insertions | Cross-tenant edge target node (when Usuário 1+ exists) | Tampering | High | Low (future) | (a) Service role inserts with hardcoded `user_id=SOVEREIGN_USER_ID` for v1; (b) For v2: validate target node `user_id` matches source observation `user_id` before insert |
> 398	| Output dir creation | Directory traversal via `--out` arg | Tampering | Medium | Low | Resolve to absolute path + reject if outside `/tmp/`, `~/`, or repo root |
> 399	| `regression` mode replay | Replay command `eval js` with malicious JS from baseline | Code Execution | **Critical** | Low (baseline trusted) | (a) Driver does NOT expose `eval` method to flows; (b) Commands.jsonl whitelist of allowed verbs (open/click/fill/snapshot/etc.); (c) Reject `eval` during replay |

> 404	**Critical (Sev × Likelihood ≥ Critical):** secret leakage via HAR/auth state. Mitigation = 4-layer defense (redactor + .gitignore + pre-commit + audit cron).
> 405	
> 406	### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions
> 407	
> 408	**Sem nova tabela, sem nova RLS policy.** Reuso de RLS existentes:
> 409	
> 410	- `mcorch_nodes` RLS é `user_id IS NULL OR auth.uid() = user_id`. Service role bypassa RLS — mas mantemos `user_id=SOVEREIGN_USER_ID` literal para não introduzir system-level nodes inadvertidamente.
> 411	- `mcorch_edges` RLS herda da política existente.
> 412	- `infra_health_logs` RLS é `Read-all for authenticated; write only service_role` — perfeitamente alinhado.
> 413	
> 414	### 5.3 JWT Validation Points
> 415	
> 416	**Nenhum endpoint próprio — não há fluxo HTTP usuário-facing.**
> 417	
> 418	JWT do Sovereign aparece apenas em:
> 419	1. **Auth state file** (`--state <path>`) — não validado pelo runner (delegado ao Chromium via agent-browser); apenas detectado expiry via HAR HTTP 401.
> 420	2. **Service role key** — não é JWT, é static secret no `.env`.
> 421

> 423	
> 424	| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy | Notes |
> 425	|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|-------|
> 426	| `SUPABASE_URL` | `mesh-persistor.ts`, `telemetry.ts` | `.env` (project root) | N/A (not secret per se) | Public URL |
> 427	| `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` | Idem | `.env` | Anual (recomendado), forced on suspected leak | NEVER em logs ou commits |
> 428	| `SOVEREIGN_USER_ID` | `mesh-persistor.ts` | `.env` | N/A (UUID estático) | Not a secret — apenas para single-tenant hardcoding |

> 430	| `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | NOT used here (embed-mcorch-node já tem) | Supabase Edge Vault | — | Embedding async via trigger, runner não usa direto |

> 434	### 5.5 LGPD Compliance Layer
> 435	
> 436	3 defesas em camadas:
> 437	
> 438	**Layer 1 — Capture-time avoidance:**
> 439	- `redactedPaths` config exclui bodies de POST a `/auth/v1/*`, `/functions/v1/*`, `/rest/v1/profiles*`, `/rest/v1/social_credentials*` (sempre que possível, evitar capturar em primeiro lugar).
> 440	
> 441	**Layer 2 — Post-capture redaction (HAR transform):**
> 442	- Regex sobre HAR JSON: substituir headers `Authorization|Cookie|apikey|x-api-key|x-supabase-*` value → `<redacted>`.
> 443	- Body redaction onde `request.url` matches `redactedPaths`.
> 444	- Cookies sanitização: keep nome do cookie + path + flags, value → `<redacted>`.
> 445	
> 446	**Layer 3 — Commit-time defense:**
> 447	- `.git/hooks/pre-commit` extension: `git diff --cached --name-only` filtrado por `e2e-output/` → executar regex secret-scan (`eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.eyJ`, `sk_(live|test)_`, `sbp_`, `whsec_`) → abort commit se hit.
> 448	
> 449	**Layer 4 — Periodic audit:**
> 450	- Cron `nightly-stage1` extension: `git grep` history para mesmo pattern; alerta via `infra_health_logs` se positivo.

> 38	export function findNewestRecordBlock(markdown: string): string | null {
> 39	  const lines = markdown.split("\n");
> 40	  let start = -1;
> 41	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {

> 208	/**
> 209	 * Parse an optional proof-manifest.json string (emitted by /handoff Phase 3)
> 210	 * into claims. Currently it contributes the handoff mesh-node UUID, which is
> 211	 * awkward to regex reliably. Malformed JSON is ignored (returns []).
> 212	 */
> 213	export function parseManifestJson(json: string): ProofClaim[] {
> 214	  const claims: ProofClaim[] = [];
> 215	  let data: unknown;
> 216	  try {
> 217	    data = JSON.parse(json);
> 218	  } catch {
> 219	    return claims;
> 220	  }
> 221	  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return claims;
> 222	  const obj = data as Record<string, unknown>;
> 223	  const nodeId = obj.handoffNodeId;
> 224	  if (typeof nodeId === "string" && UUID_RE.test(nodeId)) {
> 225	    claims.push({
> 226	      kind: "mesh_node",
> 227	      label: `handoff node ${nodeId.slice(0, 8)}`,
> 228	      expected: nodeId.toLowerCase(),
> 229	      raw: `proof-manifest.json:handoffNodeId=${nodeId}`,
> 230	      // Phase 3 structurally asserted this node — a non-resolving handoff
> 231	      // node is a genuine contradiction, so this claim is strict (can fail).
> 232	      strict: true,
> 233	    });
> 234	  }
> 235	  return claims;
> 236	}
> 237	
> 238	/**
> 239	 * Top-level extractor. `handoffMarkdown` is the full text of HANDOFF.md;
> 240	 * `manifestJson` is the optional proof-manifest.json text (hybrid input).
> 241	 */
> 242	export function extractManifest(
> 243	  handoffMarkdown: string,
> 244	  manifestJson?: string,
> 245	): ProofClaim[] {
> 246	  const block = findNewestRecordBlock(handoffMarkdown);
> 247	  const claims: ProofClaim[] = block ? extractClaimsFromBlock(block) : [];
> 248	  if (manifestJson) {
> 249	    const seen = new Set(claims.map(dedupeKey));
> 250	    for (const c of parseManifestJson(manifestJson)) {
> 251	      if (!seen.has(dedupeKey(c))) {
> 252	        seen.add(dedupeKey(c));
> 253	        claims.push(c);
> 254	      }
> 255	    }
> 256	  }
> 257	  return claims;
> 258	}

> 29	  const slash = raw.match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*\d+/);
> 30	  if (slash) return Number(slash[1]);
> 31	  const passed = raw.match(/(\d+)\s+(?:tests?\s+)?(?:passed|passam|passou)/i);
> 32	  if (passed) return Number(passed[1]);
> 33	  const bare = raw.match(/(\d+)/);
> 34	  return bare ? Number(bare[1]) : null;
> 35	}
> 36	
> 37	/** Extract the actual passing-test count from a Vitest run's stdout. */
> 38	export function parseActualTestCount(output: string): number | null {
> 39	  const tests = output.match(/Tests\s+(\d+)\s+passed/i);
> 40	  if (tests) return Number(tests[1]);
> 41	  const generic = output.match(/(\d+)\s+passed/i);
> 42	  return generic ? Number(generic[1]) : null;
> 43	}
> 44	
> 45	/** Edge-function reachability: 404 = not deployed (fail); anything else = reachable (pass). */
> 46	export function classifyEdgeStatus(status: number): "pass" | "fail" {
> 47	  return status === 404 ? "fail" : "pass";
> 48	}
> 49	
> 50	// ── Verifiers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 51	
> 52	function result(
> 53	  claim: ProofClaim,
> 54	  status: VerificationResult["status"],
> 55	  actual: string,
> 56	  detail: string,
> 57	): VerificationResult {
> 58	  return { claim, status, actual, detail };
> 59	}
> 60	
> 61	export function verifyGitCommit(claim: ProofClaim, deps: VerifierDeps): VerificationResult {
> 62	  const hash = claim.expected.trim();
> 63	  if (!isValidCommitHash(hash)) {
> 64	    return result(claim, "skip", hash, "not a valid commit-hash shape");
> 65	  }
> 66	  const exists = deps.gitObjectExists(hash);
> 67	  return exists
> 68	    ? result(claim, "pass", hash, "git object present")
> 69	    : result(claim, "fail", "absent", `no git object for ${hash}`);
> 70	}
> 71	
> 72	export async function verifyMeshRow(
> 73	  claim: ProofClaim,
> 74	  deps: VerifierDeps,
> 75	): Promise<VerificationResult> {
> 76	  const id = claim.expected.trim().toLowerCase();
> 77	  if (!isValidUuid(id)) {
> 78	    return result(claim, "skip", id, "not a valid UUID shape");
> 79	  }
> 80	  const primary = claim.kind === "mesh_edge" ? "mcorch_edges" : "mcorch_nodes";
> 81	  const secondary = claim.kind === "mesh_edge" ? "mcorch_nodes" : "mcorch_edges";
> 82	  const inPrimary = await deps.meshRowExists(primary, id);
> 83	  if (inPrimary === true) {
> 84	    return result(claim, "pass", id, `row present in ${primary}`);
> 85	  }
> 86	  // Tolerate node/edge misclassification: confirm against the other table
> 87	  // before declaring a contradiction.
> 88	  const inSecondary = await deps.meshRowExists(secondary, id);
> 89	  if (inSecondary === true) {
> 90	    return result(claim, "pass", id, `row present in ${secondary}`);
> 91	  }
> 92	  if (inPrimary === null || inSecondary === null) {
> 93	    return result(claim, "skip", "unreachable", "mcorch tables could not be queried");
> 94	  }
> 95	  // Resolved in neither table. A strict claim (e.g. from proof-manifest.json)
> 96	  // is a genuine contradiction; a prose-parsed UUID is downgraded to skip —
> 97	  // it may be a non-mesh id (user / transaction / operation).
> 98	  return claim.strict
> 99	    ? result(claim, "fail", "absent", `no row ${id} in mcorch_nodes or mcorch_edges`)
> 100	    : result(claim, "skip", "absent", `${id} is not a mesh node/edge — likely a non-mesh id`);
> 101	}
> 102	
> 103	export function verifyTsc(claim: ProofClaim, deps: VerifierDeps): VerificationResult {
> 104	  const r = deps.runTsc();
> 105	  if (r.ok) return result(claim, "pass", "0 errors", "tsc --noEmit clean");
> 106	  const firstError = r.output.split("\n").find((l) => /error TS\d+/i.test(l)) ?? "tsc reported errors";
> 107	  return result(claim, "fail", "errors", firstError.trim().slice(0, 200));
> 108	}
> 109	
> 110	export function verifyTestSuite(claim: ProofClaim, deps: VerifierDeps): VerificationResult {
> 111	  const claimed = parseClaimedTestCount(claim.expected) ?? parseClaimedTestCount(claim.raw);
> 112	  const r = deps.runTests();
> 113	  const actual = parseActualTestCount(r.output);
> 114	  if (actual === null) {
> 115	    return result(claim, "skip", "unknown", "could not parse a test count from output");
> 116	  }
> 117	  if (!r.ok) {
> 118	    return result(claim, "fail", `${actual} passed`, "test runner exited non-zero");
> 119	  }
> 120	  if (claimed !== null && actual < claimed) {
> 121	    return result(claim, "fail", `${actual} passed`, `claimed ${claimed}, ran ${actual}`);
> 122	  }
> 123	  return result(claim, "pass", `${actual} passed`, claimed !== null ? `≥ claimed ${claimed}` : "suite green");
> 124	}
> 125	
> 126	export async function verifyEdgeDeploy(
> 127	  claim: ProofClaim,
> 128	  deps: VerifierDeps,
> 129	): Promise<VerificationResult> {
> 130	  const fn = claim.expected.trim();
> 131	  if (/kb$/i.test(fn) || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/i.test(fn)) {
> 132	    return result(claim, "skip", fn, "size-only deploy claim — no function name to probe");
> 133	  }
> 134	  const status = await deps.edgeFunctionStatus(fn);
> 135	  if (status === null) {
> 136	    return result(claim, "skip", "unreachable", `could not probe /functions/v1/${fn}`);
> 137	  }
> 138	  return classifyEdgeStatus(status) === "pass"
> 139	    ? result(claim, "pass", `HTTP ${status}`, `/functions/v1/${fn} reachable`)
> 140	    : result(claim, "fail", "HTTP 404", `/functions/v1/${fn} not deployed`);
> 141	}
> 142	
> 143	export function verifyFileExists(claim: ProofClaim, deps: VerifierDeps): VerificationResult {
> 144	  const path = claim.expected.trim();
> 145	  return deps.fileExists(path)
> 146	    ? result(claim, "pass", path, "file present")
> 147	    : result(claim, "fail", "absent", `file not found: ${path}`);
> 148	}
> 149	
> 150	export function verifyOroCompliance(claim: ProofClaim): VerificationResult {
> 151	  const val = claim.expected.trim();
> 152	  if (val === "missing") {
> 153	    return result(
> 154	      claim,
> 155	      "fail",
> 156	      "missing",
> 157	      "ORO triplet (Operator, Reviewer, Owner) is missing from the record block",
> 158	    );
> 159	  }
> 160	  if (val === "malformed") {
> 161	    return result(claim, "fail", "malformed", "ORO triplet has missing or invalid/empty role definitions");
> 162	  }
> 163	  return result(claim, "pass", val, "ORO triplet declared and valid");
> 164	}
> 165	
> 166	/** Dispatch a single claim to its verifier. */
> 167	export async function verifyClaim(
> 168	  claim: ProofClaim,
> 169	  deps: VerifierDeps,
> 170	): Promise<VerificationResult> {
> 171	  switch (claim.kind) {
> 172	    case "git_commit":
> 173	      return verifyGitCommit(claim, deps);
> 174	    case "mesh_node":
> 175	    case "mesh_edge":
> 176	      return verifyMeshRow(claim, deps);
> 177	    case "tsc":
> 178	      return verifyTsc(claim, deps);
> 179	    case "test_suite":
> 180	      return verifyTestSuite(claim, deps);
> 181	    case "edge_deploy":
> 182	      return verifyEdgeDeploy(claim, deps);
> 183	    case "file_exists":
> 184	      return verifyFileExists(claim, deps);
> 185	    case "oro_compliance":
> 186	      return verifyOroCompliance(claim);
> 187	    default:
> 188	      return result(claim, "skip", "", "unknown claim kind");
> 189	  }
> 190	}
> 191	
> ```

> 1	// Type contracts for the mcorch-qa-healing material-proof audit.
> 2	// See docs/bok/mcorch-qa-healing/06-data-model.md §2 (Logical Data Model).
> 3	//
> 4	// This module has zero `node:` imports on purpose: it is reachable from the
> 5	// Vitest suite under tsconfig.app.json, which restricts global @types via
> 6	// `types: ["vitest/globals"]`.
> 7	
> 8	export type ClaimKind =
> 9	  | "git_commit"
> 10	  | "mesh_node"
> 11	  | "mesh_edge"
> 12	  | "tsc"
> 13	  | "test_suite"
> 14	  | "edge_deploy"
> 15	  | "file_exists"
> 16	  | "oro_compliance";
> 17	
> 18	/** A single material-proof assertion extracted from a /handoff seal. */
> 19	export interface ProofClaim {
> 20	  kind: ClaimKind;
> 21	  label: string;
> 22	  /** The asserted artifact: a hash, UUID, count, function name, or path. */
> 23	  expected: string;
> 24	  /** The original HANDOFF.md text the claim was parsed from. */
> 25	  raw: string;
> 26	  /**
> 27	   * When true, a non-resolving claim is a hard `fail` (blocks the seal). When
> 28	   * false/absent the verifier downgrades a non-resolving claim to `skip`.
> 29	   * Set for unambiguous claims (e.g. a mesh UUID from proof-manifest.json);
> 30	   * left unset for prose-parsed UUIDs, which may be non-mesh ids.
> 31	   */
> 32	  strict?: boolean;
> 33	}
> 34	
> 35	export type VerdictStatus = "pass" | "fail" | "skip";
> 36	
> 37	/** The outcome of verifying one ProofClaim against its physical artifact. */
> 38	export interface VerificationResult {
> 39	  claim: ProofClaim;
> 40	  status: VerdictStatus;
> 41	  actual: string;
> 42	  detail: string;
> 43	}
> 44	
> 45	/** The aggregate result of auditing one seal. */
> 46	export interface AuditOutcome {
> 47	  results: VerificationResult[];
> 48	  verdict: "allowed" | "blocked";
> 49	  markdown: string;
> 50	}
> 51	
> 52	export interface CommandResult {
> 53	  ok: boolean;
> 54	  output: string;
> 55	}
> 56	
> 57	/**
> 58	 * Dependency seam for the verifiers. Real implementations (git / fetch / fs)
> 59	 * live in run-audit.ts; unit tests inject fakes. Keeping the I/O behind this
> 60	 * interface is what lets verifiers.ts stay free of `node:` imports.
> 61	 */
> 62	export interface VerifierDeps {
> 63	  /** True iff `git cat-file -e <hash>^{commit}` succeeds. */
> 64	  gitObjectExists(hash: string): boolean;
> 65	  /** Re-run `npx tsc --noEmit`. */
> 66	  runTsc(): CommandResult;
> 67	  /** Re-run `bun run test`. */
> 68	  runTests(): CommandResult;
> 69	  /** True / false if the row exists; null if Supabase could not be reached. */
> 70	  meshRowExists(table: "mcorch_nodes" | "mcorch_edges", id: string): Promise<boolean | null>;
> 71	  /** HTTP status of GET /functions/v1/<fn>; null if it could not be reached. */
> 72	  edgeFunctionStatus(fnName: string): Promise<number | null>;
> 73	  /** True iff the path exists on disk (resolved from repo root). */
> 74	  fileExists(relPath: string): boolean;
> 75	}
> 76	
> 77	export type HealthStatus = "healthy" | "degraded" | "failed";
> 78	
> 79	export interface QaEnv {
> 80	  supabaseUrl: string;
> 81	  serviceRoleKey: string;
> 82	}
> 83	
> ```

> 1	// Material Proof Audit — entrypoint — mcorch-qa-healing FR-008.
> 2	//
> 3	//   bun run scripts/qa/run-audit.ts [handoffPath=HANDOFF.md]
> 4	//
> 5	// This is the only module in scripts/qa/ that touches the filesystem, git,
> 6	// child processes and the network. It builds the real VerifierDeps and feeds
> 7	// the pure modules (extract-manifest, verifiers). It is not imported by any
> 8	// src/ file, so it is outside the tsc graph; Bun runs it directly.
> 9	//
> 10	// Exit code:  0 = verdict 'allowed'  ·  1 = verdict 'blocked'.
> 11	
> 12	import { execSync, execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
> 13	import fs from "node:fs";
> 14	import path from "node:path";
> 15	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
> 16	
> 17	import { extractManifest } from "./extract-manifest";
> 18	import { verifyClaim } from "./verifiers";
> 19	import { writeHealth } from "./health-pulse";
> 20	import type {
> 21	  AuditOutcome,
> 22	  CommandResult,
> 23	  HealthStatus,
> 24	  QaEnv,
> 25	  VerificationResult,
> 26	  VerifierDeps,
> 27	} from "./types";
> 28	
> 29	const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
> 30	const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");
> 31	
> 32	// ── Environment ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 33	
> 34	function loadEnv(): QaEnv {
> 35	  const envPath = path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, ".env");
> 36	  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
> 37	    for (const line of fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8").split("\n")) {
> 38	      const m = line.match(/^([^=]+)=(.*)$/);
> 39	      if (m) {
> 40	        const key = m[1].trim();
> 41	        if (!process.env[key]) {
> 42	          process.env[key] = m[2].trim().replace(/^['"](.*)['"]$/, "$1");
> 43	        }
> 44	      }
> 45	    }
> 46	  }
> 47	  return {
> 48	    supabaseUrl: process.env.SUPABASE_URL || process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL || "",
> 49	    serviceRoleKey: process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY || "",
> 50	  };
> 51	}
> 52	
> 53	// ── Real dependency implementations ─────────────────────────────────────────
> 54	
> 55	function runCommand(cmd: string): CommandResult {
> 56	  try {
> 57	    const output = execSync(cmd, {
> 58	      cwd: REPO_ROOT,
> 59	      encoding: "utf8",
> 60	      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
> 61	    });
> 62	    return { ok: true, output };
> 63	  } catch (e: unknown) {
> 64	    const err = e as { stdout?: string; stderr?: string; message?: string };
> 65	    const output = `${err.stdout ?? ""}${err.stderr ?? ""}` || err.message || String(e);
> 66	    return { ok: false, output };
> 67	  }
> 68	}
> 69	
> 70	function buildDeps(env: QaEnv): VerifierDeps {
> 71	  const headers = {
> 72	    apikey: env.serviceRoleKey,
> 73	    Authorization: `Bearer ${env.serviceRoleKey}`,
> 74	  };
> 75	  return {
> 76	    gitObjectExists(hash: string): boolean {
> 77	      try {
> 78	        execFileSync("git", ["cat-file", "-e", `${hash}^{commit}`], {
> 79	          cwd: REPO_ROOT,
> 80	          stdio: "ignore",
> 81	        });
> 82	        return true;
> 83	      } catch {
> 84	        return false;
> 85	      }
> 86	    },
> 87	    runTsc(): CommandResult {
> 88	      return runCommand("npx tsc --noEmit");
> 89	    },
> 90	    runTests(): CommandResult {
> 91	      return runCommand("bun run test");
> 92	    },
> 93	    async meshRowExists(table, id): Promise<boolean | null> {
> 94	      if (!env.supabaseUrl || !env.serviceRoleKey) return null;
> 95	      try {
> 96	        const res = await fetch(
> 97	          `${env.supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/${table}?id=eq.${id}&select=id`,
> 98	          { headers },
> 99	        );
> 100	        if (!res.ok) return null;
> 101	        const rows = (await res.json()) as unknown;
> 102	        return Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length > 0;
> 103	      } catch {
> 104	        return null;
> 105	      }
> 106	    },
> 107	    async edgeFunctionStatus(fnName): Promise<number | null> {
> 108	      if (!env.supabaseUrl) return null;
> 109	      try {
> 110	        const res = await fetch(`${env.supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/${fnName}`, {
> 111	          method: "GET",
> 112	          headers,
> 113	        });
> 114	        return res.status;
> 115	      } catch {
> 116	        return null;
> 117	      }
> 118	    },
> 119	    fileExists(relPath: string): boolean {
> 120	      return fs.existsSync(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, relPath));
> 121	    },
> 122	  };
> 123	}
> 124	
> 125	// ── Rendering ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 126	
> 127	function glyph(status: VerificationResult["status"]): string {
> 128	  if (status === "pass") return "✅";
> 129	  if (status === "fail") return "🔴";
> 130	  return "⏭";
> 131	}
> 132	
> 133	function cell(value: string): string {
> 134	  return String(value).replace(/\|/g, "/").replace(/\n/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 90);
> 135	}
> 136	
> 137	function render(results: VerificationResult[]): { markdown: string; verdict: "allowed" | "blocked" } {
> 138	  const pass = results.filter((r) => r.status === "pass").length;
> 139	  const skip = results.filter((r) => r.status === "skip").length;
> 140	  const fail = results.filter((r) => r.status === "fail").length;
> 141	  const verdict: "allowed" | "blocked" = fail > 0 ? "blocked" : "allowed";
> 142	
> 143	  const lines: string[] = ["### 📋 Proof Manifest — Material Proof Audit (Phase 5c)", ""];
> 144	
> 145	  if (results.length === 0) {
> 146	    lines.push(
> 147	      "> ⚠️ No material-proof claims were extracted from the newest HANDOFF.md Record block.",
> 148	      "> Confirm the Record exists and follows the documented format (FMEA-004).",
> 149	      "",
> 150	      "QA VERDICT: 0 verified · 0 skipped · 0 failed → SEAL ALLOWED (no claims)",
> 151	    );
> 152	    return { markdown: lines.join("\n"), verdict: "allowed" };
> 153	  }
> 154	
> 155	  lines.push("| Claim | Kind | Expected | Actual | Verdict |", "|-------|------|----------|--------|---------|");
> 156	  for (const r of results) {
> 157	    lines.push(
> 158	      `| ${cell(r.claim.label)} | ${r.claim.kind} | ${cell(r.claim.expected)} | ${cell(r.actual)} | ${glyph(r.status)} |`,
> 159	    );
> 160	  }
> 161	  lines.push("");
> 162	  for (const r of results.filter((x) => x.status === "fail")) {
> 163	    lines.push(`- 🔴 **${cell(r.claim.label)}** — ${cell(r.detail)}`);
> 164	  }
> 165	  if (fail > 0) lines.push("");
> 166	  lines.push(
> 167	    `QA VERDICT: ${pass} verified · ${skip} skipped · ${fail} failed → SEAL ${verdict === "blocked" ? "BLOCKED" : "ALLOWED"}`,
> 168	  );
> 169	  return { markdown: lines.join("\n"), verdict };
> 170	}
> 171	
> 172	// ── Audit orchestration ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 173	
> 174	/**
> 175	 * Run the full material-proof audit for a seal. Exported so the OpenClaw cron
> 176	 * (or any programmatic caller) can consume the structured outcome directly.
> 177	 */
> 178	export async function runAudit(handoffPath?: string): Promise<AuditOutcome> {
> 179	  const env = loadEnv();
> 180	  const deps = buildDeps(env);
> 181	
> 182	  const resolvedHandoff = path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, handoffPath ?? "HANDOFF.md");
> 183	  const handoffText = fs.existsSync(resolvedHandoff)
> 184	    ? fs.readFileSync(resolvedHandoff, "utf8")
> 185	    : "";
> 186	
> 187	  const manifestPath = path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, "proof-manifest.json");
> 188	  const manifestJson = fs.existsSync(manifestPath)
> 189	    ? fs.readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8")
> 190	    : undefined;
> 191	
> 192	  const claims = extractManifest(handoffText, manifestJson);
> 193	
> 194	  const results: VerificationResult[] = [];
> 195	  for (const claim of claims) {
> 196	    results.push(await verifyClaim(claim, deps));
> 197	  }
> 198	
> 199	  const { markdown, verdict } = render(results);
> 200	
> 201	  const skips = results.filter((r) => r.status === "skip").length;
> 202	  const health: HealthStatus =
> 203	    verdict === "blocked" ? "failed" : skips > 0 || results.length === 0 ? "degraded" : "healthy";
> 204	  await writeHealth(env, health);
> 205	
> 206	  return { results, verdict, markdown };
> 207	}
> 208	
> 209	// ── CLI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 210	
> 211	async function mainCli(): Promise<void> {
> 212	  const started = Date.now();
> 213	  const outcome = await runAudit(process.argv[2]);
> 214	  process.stdout.write(`${outcome.markdown}\n`);
> 215	  process.stdout.write(`\n(audit completed in ${((Date.now() - started) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)\n`);
> 216	  process.exit(outcome.verdict === "blocked" ? 1 : 0);
> 217	}
> 218	
> 219	const invokedDirectly = typeof process.argv[1] === "string" && process.argv[1].includes("run-audit");
> 220	if (invokedDirectly) {
> 221	  void mainCli();
> 222	}
> 223	
> ```

  "content": "// Finding Schema v1.0.0 \u2014 e2e-user-zero\n//\n// Pure TypeScript types: no imports, no side effects. Reachable by both the\n// runtime layer (runner / driver / classifier) and any external consumer that\n// wants to parse `findings.json`. The `schema_version` literal is hard-coded\n// to \"1.0.0\" to make breaking changes explicit (FR-E2E-019).\n\nexport type Severity = \"P0\" | \"P1\" | \"P2\" | \"P3\";\n\nexport type IssueType =\n  | \"bug\"\n  | \"ux\"\n  | \"perf\"\n  | \"accessibility\"\n  | \"security\"\n  | \"regression\";\n\nexport type FlowMode = \"dogfood\" | \"focused\" | \"regression\";\n\n/** Console message captured from agent-browser. */\nexport interface ConsoleMessage {\n  type: \"log\" | \"info\" | \"warn\" | \"error\" | \"debug\";\n  text: string;\n  source?: { url: string; line: number; column: number };\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\n/** Network request captured from HAR / get network. MVP: minimal projection. */\nexport interface NetworkRequest {\n  url: string;\n  method: string;\n  status: number;\n  durationMs?: number;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\n/** Accessibility snapshot result (a11y tree + raw text). */\nexport interface SnapshotResult {\n  page: { title: string; url: string };\n  text: string;\n  raw_command_output: string;\n}\n\n/** Screenshot reference with provenance for evidence trail. */\nexport interface ScreenshotRef {\n  path: string;\n  ts: number;\n  step: string;\n}\n\n/** Flow execution context \u2014 injected by runner into each flow. */\nexport interface FlowContext {\n  runId: string;\n  outputDir: string;\n  authStatePath?: string;\n  mode: FlowMode;\n}\n\n/** What every flow returns to the runner for classification. */\nexport interface FlowResult {\n  flow: string;\n  consoleMessages: ConsoleMessage[];\n  networkRequests: NetworkRequest[];\n  screenshots: ScreenshotRef[];\n  ended_at: string;\n}\n\n/** A single finding produced by the classifier. */\nexport interface Finding {\n  id: string;\n  flow: string;\n  severity: Severity;\n  issue_type: IssueType;\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  repro_steps: string[];\n  evidence_paths: {\n    screenshot?: string;\n    har_snippet?: string;\n    console_snippet?: string;\n    snapshot_ref?: string;\n  };\n  affected_module?: { node_id?: string; node_name?: string; path?: string };\n  suggested_fix?: string;\n  detected_at: string;\n}\n\n/** Classifier input \u2014 all four surfaces captured for a single flow. */\nexport interface ClassifyInput {\n  flow: string;\n  consoleMessages: ConsoleMessage[];\n  networkRequests: NetworkRequest[];\n  screenshots: ScreenshotRef[];\n}\n\n/** Severity histogram for a single run. */\nexport interface RunSummary {\n  p0_count: number;\n  p1_count: number;\n  p2_count: number;\n  p3_count: number;\n  total: number;\n}\n\n/** Top-level `findings.json` schema (FR-E2E-019). */\nexport interface FindingsReport {\n  schema_version: \"1.0.0\";\n  run_id: string;\n  mode: FlowMode;\n  flows: string[];\n  run_started_at: string;\n  run_ended_at: string;\n  summary: RunSummary;\n  findings: Finding[];\n}\n"
}
