{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/lib/classifier.ts",
  "content": "// Findings Classifier \u2014 e2e-user-zero FR-E2E-007 (deterministic heuristics).\n//\n// Pure function: same input \u2192 same findings, no clock-dependent except for the\n// `detected_at` ISO string (injectable via `now` parameter for unit testing).\n//\n// MVP heuristics:\n//   (a) HTTP 4xx/5xx network responses \u2192 bug findings.\n//   (b) console.error / console.warn \u2192 bug findings.\n//   (c) (deferred \u2014 snapshot / black-canvas detection requires HAR + visual diff.)\n\nimport type {\n  ClassifyInput,\n  ConsoleMessage,\n  Finding,\n  IssueType,\n  NetworkRequest,\n  Severity,\n} from \"./finding-schema\";\n\n/** Classify an HTTP status into severity + issue type, or null if not a finding. */\nexport function classifyHttpStatus(\n  status: number,\n): { severity: Severity; issue_type: IssueType } | null {\n  if (status >= 500) return { severity: \"P0\", issue_type: \"bug\" };\n  if (status === 401) return { severity: \"P0\", issue_type: \"security\" };\n  if (status === 404) return { severity: \"P1\", issue_type: \"bug\" };\n  if (status >= 400) return { severity: \"P1\", issue_type: \"bug\" };\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Classify a console message type into severity + issue type, or null. */\nexport function classifyConsoleType(\n  type: ConsoleMessage[\"type\"],\n): { severity: Severity; issue_type: IssueType } | null {\n  if (type === \"error\") return { severity: \"P1\", issue_type: \"bug\" };\n  if (type === \"warn\") return { severity: \"P3\", issue_type: \"bug\" };\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction findingId(flow: string, index: number): string {\n  return `F-${flow}-${String(index + 1).padStart(3, \"0\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction shortenUrl(url: string): string {\n  try {\n    const u = new URL(url);\n    return `${u.pathname}${u.search}`.slice(0, 80);\n  } catch {\n    return url.slice(0, 80);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction truncate(s: string, n: number): string {\n  return s.length > n ? `${s.slice(0, n - 1)}\u2026` : s;\n}\n\nfunction classifyNetwork(\n  flow: string,\n  req: NetworkRequest,\n  index: number,\n  now: string,\n): Finding | null {\n  const verdict = classifyHttpStatus(req.status);\n  if (!verdict) return null;\n  return {\n    id: findingId(flow, index),\n    flow,\n    severity: verdict.severity,\n    issue_type: verdict.issue_type,\n    title: `HTTP ${req.status} on ${req.method} ${shortenUrl(req.url)}`,\n    description: `Unexpected HTTP ${req.status} during flow \\`${flow}\\`. URL: ${req.url}`,\n    repro_steps: [\n      `Open the flow \\`${flow}\\``,\n      `Observe network request: ${req.method} ${req.url}`,\n    ],\n    evidence_paths: {},\n    detected_at: now,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction classifyConsole(\n  flow: string,\n  msg: ConsoleMessage,\n  index: number,\n  now: string,\n): Finding | null {\n  const verdict = classifyConsoleType(msg.type);\n  if (!verdict) return null;\n  return {\n    id: findingId(flow, index),\n    flow,\n    severity: verdict.severity,\n    issue_type: verdict.issue_type,\n    title: `console.${msg.type}: ${truncate(msg.text, 80)}`,\n    description: msg.text,\n    repro_steps: [\n      `Open the flow \\`${flow}\\``,\n      `Observe console messages`,\n    ],\n    evidence_paths: msg.source\n      ? { console_snippet: `${msg.source.url}:${msg.source.line}` }\n      : {},\n    detected_at: now,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Classify a flow's captured surfaces into a deterministic list of findings.\n * The optional `now` argument lets tests pin the timestamp.\n */\nexport function classify(\n  input: ClassifyInput,\n  now: string = new Date().toISOString(),\n): Finding[] {\n  const findings: Finding[] = [];\n\n  for (const req of input.networkRequests) {\n    const f = classifyNetwork(input.flow, req, findings.length, now);\n    if (f) findings.push(f);\n  }\n\n  for (const msg of input.consoleMessages) {\n    const f = classifyConsole(input.flow, msg, findings.length, now);\n    if (f) findings.push(f);\n  }\n\n  return findings;\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "// Browser Driver \u2014 e2e-user-zero FR-E2E-001 .. FR-E2E-004 + FR-E2E-020.\n//\n// Thin wrapper over the `agent-browser` CLI (v0.27.0+). Each public method\n// shells out via node:child_process.spawnSync \u2014 synchronous on purpose to\n// keep flow code straight-line and free of async-await noise (the FRD \u00a74.2\n// types declare async; we run sync here for MVP simplicity \u2014 downstream code\n// can still `await driver.open(...)` because we wrap returns in Promises when\n// needed, but flows in this MVP call methods synchronously).\n//\n// All sensitive arguments (auth-state paths, Bearer tokens) are scrubbed\n// before `commands.jsonl` write \u2014 FR-E2E-020 sensitive-arg scrub.\n\nimport { spawnSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { appendFileSync, writeFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\n\nimport type {\n  ConsoleMessage,\n  NetworkRequest,\n  SnapshotResult,\n} from \"./finding-schema\";\n\nexport interface BrowserDriver {\n  open(url: string): void;\n  click(refOrSelector: string): void;\n  fill(refOrSelector: string, text: string): void;\n  press(key: string): void;\n  wait(opts: WaitOpts): void;\n  screenshot(path: string): void;\n  snapshot(): SnapshotResult;\n  evalJs(js: string): string;\n  getConsoleMessages(): ConsoleMessage[];\n  getNetworkRequests(): NetworkRequest[];\n  close(): void;\n  getCommands(): readonly DriverCommand[];\n}\n\nexport interface WaitOpts {\n  ms?: number;\n  selector?: string;\n  load?: \"networkidle\" | \"domcontent\";\n}\n\nexport interface DriverCommand {\n  ts: number;\n  command: string;\n  args: string[];\n  exitCode: number;\n  stdoutBrief: string;\n}\n\nexport interface DriverOptions {\n  sessionName: string;\n  authStatePath?: string;\n  commandsLogPath?: string;\n  headless?: boolean;\n  binPath?: string;\n  timeoutMs?: number;\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_BIN = \"agent-browser\";\nconst DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000;\n\nexport class BrowserDriverError extends Error {\n  constructor(message: string) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = \"BrowserDriverError\";\n  }\n}\n\nexport class BrowserDriverImpl implements BrowserDriver {\n  private readonly sessionName: string;\n  private readonly authStatePath?: string;\n  private readonly commandsLogPath?: string;\n  private readonly headless: boolean;\n  private readonly bin: string;\n  private readonly timeoutMs: number;\n  private readonly commands: DriverCommand[] = [];\n\n  constructor(opts: DriverOptions) {\n    this.sessionName = opts.sessionName;\n    this.authStatePath = opts.authStatePath;\n    this.commandsLogPath = opts.commandsLogPath;\n    this.headless = opts.headless ?? true;\n    this.bin = opts.binPath ?? DEFAULT_BIN;\n    this.timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;\n    if (this.commandsLogPath) {\n      writeFileSync(this.commandsLogPath, \"\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Sensitive arg scrubber \u2014 keeps log free of tokens / state paths. */\n  private scrubArg(arg: string): string {\n    if (this.authStatePath && arg === this.authStatePath) return \"<auth-state>\";\n    if (/^Bearer\\s+/i.test(arg)) return \"<bearer-redacted>\";\n    if (/^(eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ|sk_(live|test)_|sbp_|whsec_)/.test(arg)) {\n      return \"<secret-redacted>\";\n    }\n    return arg;\n  }\n\n  private exec(args: string[]): { stdout: string; stderr: string; exitCode: number } {\n    const fullArgs = [\"--session\", this.sessionName, ...args];\n    const res = spawnSync(this.bin, fullArgs, {\n      encoding: \"utf8\",\n      timeout: this.timeoutMs,\n    });\n    const stdout = res.stdout ?? \"\";\n    const stderr = res.stderr ?? \"\";\n    const exitCode = res.status ?? -1;\n\n    const cmd: DriverCommand = {\n      ts: Date.now(),\n      command: args[0] ?? \"<empty>\",\n      args: args.slice(1).map((a) => this.scrubArg(a)),\n      exitCode,\n      stdoutBrief: stdout.slice(0, 200),\n    };\n    this.commands.push(cmd);\n    if (this.commandsLogPath) {\n      appendFileSync(this.commandsLogPath, `${JSON.stringify(cmd)}\\n`);\n    }\n\n    if (exitCode !== 0) {\n      const detail = (stderr.trim() || stdout.trim()).slice(0, 240);\n      throw new BrowserDriverError(\n        `agent-browser ${args.slice(0, 2).join(\" \")} exited ${exitCode}: ${detail}`,\n      );\n    }\n    return { stdout, stderr, exitCode };\n  }\n\n  open(url: string): void {\n    const args = [\"open\", url];\n    if (this.authStatePath) args.push(\"--state\", this.authStatePath);\n    if (!this.headless) args.push(\"--headed\");\n    this.exec(args);\n  }\n\n  click(refOrSelector: string): void {\n    this.exec([\"click\", refOrSelector]);\n  }\n\n  fill(refOrSelector: string, text: string): void {\n    this.exec([\"fill\", refOrSelector, text]);\n  }\n\n  press(key: string): void {\n    this.exec([\"press\", key]);\n  }\n\n  wait(opts: WaitOpts): void {\n    if (typeof opts.ms === \"number\") {\n      this.exec([\"wait\", String(opts.ms)]);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (opts.selector) {\n      this.exec([\"wait\", opts.selector]);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (opts.load === \"networkidle\") {\n      this.exec([\"wait\", \"networkidle\"]);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (opts.load === \"domcontent\") {\n      this.exec([\"wait\", \"domcontent\"]);\n      return;\n    }\n    throw new BrowserDriverError(\"wait() called without ms / selector / load\");\n  }\n\n  screenshot(path: string): void {\n    this.exec([\"screenshot\", path]);\n  }\n\n  snapshot(): SnapshotResult {\n    const { stdout } = this.exec([\"snapshot\"]);\n    return {\n      page: { title: \"\", url: \"\" },\n      text: stdout,\n      raw_command_output: stdout,\n    };\n  }\n\n  evalJs(js: string): string {\n    const { stdout } = this.exec([\"eval\", js]);\n    return stdout.trim();\n  }\n\n  getConsoleMessages(): ConsoleMessage[] {\n    // Best-effort: agent-browser `get console-messages` JSON output, if\n    // supported. Failures are tolerated \u2014 flows survive without console data.\n    try {\n      const { stdout } = this.exec([\"get\", \"console-messages\"]);\n      const out: ConsoleMessage[] = [];\n      for (const line of stdout.split(\"\\n\")) {\n        const trimmed = line.trim();\n        if (!trimmed) continue;\n        try {\n          const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed) as ConsoleMessage;\n          if (parsed && typeof parsed.text === \"string\") out.push(parsed);\n        } catch {\n          // skip non-JSON line\n        }\n      }\n      return out;\n    } catch {\n      return [];\n    }\n  }\n\n  getNetworkRequests(): NetworkRequest[] {\n    // MVP: HAR parsing deferred to next session (FR-E2E-002 is sealed in BoK\n    // but the redactor + HAR parser are next-iteration scope).\n    return [];\n  }\n\n  close(): void {\n    try {\n      this.exec([\"close\"]);\n    } catch {\n      // tolerated \u2014 close failures during cleanup must not crash the runner\n    }\n  }\n\n  getCommands(): readonly DriverCommand[] {\n    return this.commands;\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "// Report Renderer \u2014 e2e-user-zero (markdown report + findings.json builder).\n//\n// Pure: takes findings + run metadata, returns a `FindingsReport` value and\n// the corresponding markdown. No I/O \u2014 the runner writes the files.\n//\n// The \"\u2705 GO\" / \"\ud83d\udd34 NO-GO\" badge string is the contract the Phase 5c gate\n// verifier (verifyE2EUserZero) greps for. DO NOT change those literals\n// without bumping `schema_version` and updating verifiers.ts.\n\nimport type {\n  Finding,\n  FindingsReport,\n  FlowMode,\n  RunSummary,\n} from \"./finding-schema\";\n\nexport const GO_BADGE = \"\u2705 GO\";\nexport const NO_GO_BADGE = \"\ud83d\udd34 NO-GO\";\n\n/** Severity histogram over a findings list. */\nexport function buildSummary(findings: Finding[]): RunSummary {\n  let p0 = 0;\n  let p1 = 0;\n  let p2 = 0;\n  let p3 = 0;\n  for (const f of findings) {\n    if (f.severity === \"P0\") p0++;\n    else if (f.severity === \"P1\") p1++;\n    else if (f.severity === \"P2\") p2++;\n    else if (f.severity === \"P3\") p3++;\n  }\n  return { p0_count: p0, p1_count: p1, p2_count: p2, p3_count: p3, total: findings.length };\n}\n\nexport interface BuildReportInput {\n  runId: string;\n  mode: FlowMode;\n  flows: string[];\n  startedAt: string;\n  endedAt: string;\n  findings: Finding[];\n}\n\nexport function buildReport(input: BuildReportInput): FindingsReport {\n  return {\n    schema_version: \"1.0.0\",\n    run_id: input.runId,\n    mode: input.mode,\n    flows: input.flows,\n    run_started_at: input.startedAt,\n    run_ended_at: input.endedAt,\n    summary: buildSummary(input.findings),\n    findings: input.findings,\n  };\n}\n\n/** GO when zero P0 AND zero P1 \u2014 anything else NO-GO. */\nexport function verdictFor(summary: RunSummary): \"GO\" | \"NO-GO\" {\n  return summary.p0_count === 0 && summary.p1_count === 0 ? \"GO\" : \"NO-GO\";\n}\n\nexport function renderMarkdown(report: FindingsReport): string {\n  const { summary, findings } = report;\n  const verdict = verdictFor(summary);\n  const badge = verdict === \"GO\" ? GO_BADGE : NO_GO_BADGE;\n\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  lines.push(`# e2e-user-zero \u2014 Run \\`${report.run_id}\\``);\n  lines.push(\"\");\n  lines.push(`**Verdict:** ${badge}`);\n  lines.push(`**Mode:** \\`${report.mode}\\`  `);\n  lines.push(\n    `**Flows:** ${\n      report.flows.length > 0\n        ? report.flows.map((f) => `\\`${f}\\``).join(\", \")\n        : \"_none_\"\n    }`,\n  );\n  lines.push(`**Started:** \\`${report.run_started_at}\\`  `);\n  lines.push(`**Ended:** \\`${report.run_ended_at}\\``);\n  lines.push(\"\");\n  lines.push(\"## Summary\");\n  lines.push(\"\");\n  lines.push(\"| Severity | Count |\");\n  lines.push(\"|----------|-------|\");\n  lines.push(`| P0       | ${summary.p0_count} |`);\n  lines.push(`| P1       | ${summary.p1_count} |`);\n  lines.push(`| P2       | ${summary.p2_count} |`);\n  lines.push(`| P3       | ${summary.p3_count} |`);\n  lines.push(`| **Total** | **${summary.total}** |`);\n  lines.push(\"\");\n\n  lines.push(\"## Findings\");\n  lines.push(\"\");\n  if (findings.length === 0) {\n    lines.push(\"_No findings detected. Flow surfaces executed cleanly._\");\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  } else {\n    for (const f of findings) {\n      lines.push(`### \\`${f.id}\\` \u2014 [${f.severity} \u00b7 ${f.issue_type}] ${f.title}`);\n      lines.push(\"\");\n      lines.push(`- **Flow:** \\`${f.flow}\\``);\n      lines.push(`- **Detected:** \\`${f.detected_at}\\``);\n      lines.push(\"\");\n      lines.push(f.description);\n      lines.push(\"\");\n      if (f.repro_steps.length > 0) {\n        lines.push(\"**Repro steps:**\");\n        for (const s of f.repro_steps) {\n          lines.push(`1. ${s}`);\n        }\n        lines.push(\"\");\n      }\n      if (f.evidence_paths.screenshot) {\n        lines.push(`**Screenshot:** \\`${f.evidence_paths.screenshot}\\``);\n        lines.push(\"\");\n      }\n      if (f.evidence_paths.console_snippet) {\n        lines.push(`**Console source:** \\`${f.evidence_paths.console_snippet}\\``);\n        lines.push(\"\");\n      }\n      if (f.suggested_fix) {\n        lines.push(`**Suggested fix:** ${f.suggested_fix}`);\n        lines.push(\"\");\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  lines.push(\"---\");\n  lines.push(\"\");\n  lines.push(\n    `_Generated by e2e-user-zero runner \u2014 schema \\`${report.schema_version}\\`_`,\n  );\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/flows/auth-login.ts",
  "content": "// Flow \u2014 auth-login \u2014 e2e-user-zero MVP.\n//\n// The minimal user journey: hydrate the auth landing page on\n// `https://login.mcorch.com/auth` and verify the page renders without\n// console errors / 4xx-5xx network responses. This MVP flow does NOT\n// fill credentials (the BoK assumes the credentialed flow runs under\n// `--state` in dogfood mode). It exists primarily to validate the runner\n// skeleton end-to-end and to provide a fast Phase 5c sanity check for\n// any change touching authentication surfaces.\n//\n// FR-E2E-006 contract: default export is a synchronous function taking\n// the driver instance and the FlowContext.\n\nimport path from \"node:path\";\n\nimport type { BrowserDriver } from \"../lib/browser-driver\";\nimport type {\n  ConsoleMessage,\n  FlowContext,\n  FlowResult,\n  NetworkRequest,\n  ScreenshotRef,\n} from \"../lib/finding-schema\";\n\nconst AUTH_URL = \"https://login.mcorch.com/auth\";\nconst HYDRATION_WAIT_MS = 2000;\nconst FLOW_NAME = \"auth-login\";\n\nexport default function authLoginFlow(\n  driver: BrowserDriver,\n  ctx: FlowContext,\n): FlowResult {\n  const screenshots: ScreenshotRef[] = [];\n\n  // Step 1 \u2014 navigate\n  driver.open(AUTH_URL);\n  driver.wait({ load: \"networkidle\" });\n  driver.wait({ ms: HYDRATION_WAIT_MS });\n\n  // Step 2 \u2014 capture landing state\n  const initialPath = path.join(\n    ctx.outputDir,\n    \"screenshots\",\n    `${FLOW_NAME}-01-initial.png`,\n  );\n  driver.screenshot(initialPath);\n  screenshots.push({ path: initialPath, ts: Date.now(), step: \"initial\" });\n\n  // Step 3 \u2014 capture final state (placeholder; richer assertions belong in\n  // next iteration when --state is wired into a credentialed sub-flow)\n  const finalPath = path.join(\n    ctx.outputDir,\n    \"screenshots\",\n    `${FLOW_NAME}-02-final.png`,\n  );\n  driver.screenshot(finalPath);\n  screenshots.push({ path: finalPath, ts: Date.now(), step: \"final\" });\n\n  // Step 4 \u2014 drain observability surfaces (best-effort)\n  let consoleMessages: ConsoleMessage[] = [];\n  try {\n    consoleMessages = driver.getConsoleMessages();\n  } catch {\n    // tolerated \u2014 capture failures must not abort the flow\n  }\n  const networkRequests: NetworkRequest[] = driver.getNetworkRequests();\n\n  return {\n    flow: FLOW_NAME,\n    consoleMessages,\n    networkRequests,\n    screenshots,\n    ended_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bun\n// e2e-user-zero \u2014 CLI runner (FR-E2E-005 / FR-E2E-006 / FR-E2E-017 / FR-E2E-018).\n//\n// MVP scope: focused mode + auth-login flow + Phase 5c artifact emission.\n// Dogfood and regression modes are scaffolded but reject with exit 4 \u2014 they\n// land in the next iteration along with the remaining 6 default flows\n// (trigger-orchestration, dashboard-tour, etc.), the LGPD redactor, the\n// mesh-persistor, and the runner self-test.\n\nimport {\n  appendFileSync,\n  existsSync,\n  mkdirSync,\n  readFileSync,\n  writeFileSync,\n} from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nimport authLoginFlow from \"./flows/auth-login\";\nimport {\n  BrowserDriverError,\n  BrowserDriverImpl,\n  type BrowserDriver,\n} from \"./lib/browser-driver\";\nimport { classify } from \"./lib/classifier\";\nimport type { Finding, FlowContext, FlowMode, FlowResult } from \"./lib/finding-schema\";\nimport { buildReport, renderMarkdown } from \"./lib/report-renderer\";\n\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"../../..\");\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Flow registry (FR-E2E-006: static whitelist \u2014 no glob discovery) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ntype FlowFn = (driver: BrowserDriver, ctx: FlowContext) => FlowResult;\nconst FLOWS: Record<string, FlowFn> = {\n  \"auth-login\": authLoginFlow,\n};\n\n// \u2500\u2500 CLI argument parsing \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ninterface CliArgs {\n  mode: FlowMode;\n  flow?: string;\n  baseline?: string;\n  state?: string;\n  out?: string;\n  json: boolean;\n  help: boolean;\n  headed: boolean;\n}\n\ntype ParseResult = { ok: true; args: CliArgs } | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nfunction parseArgs(argv: string[]): ParseResult {\n  const args: CliArgs = {\n    mode: \"focused\",\n    json: false,\n    help: false,\n    headed: false,\n  };\n  for (let i = 2; i < argv.length; i++) {\n    const a = argv[i];\n    switch (a) {\n      case \"--help\":\n      case \"-h\":\n        args.help = true;\n        break;\n      case \"--mode\": {\n        const v = argv[++i];\n        if (v !== \"dogfood\" && v !== \"focused\" && v !== \"regression\") {\n          return {\n            ok: false,\n            error: \"Modo inv\u00e1lido \u2014 use dogfood, focused ou regression\",\n          };\n        }\n        args.mode = v;\n        break;\n      }\n      case \"--flow\":\n        args.flow = argv[++i];\n        break;\n      case \"--baseline\":\n        args.baseline = argv[++i];\n        break;\n      case \"--state\":\n        args.state = argv[++i];\n        break;\n      case \"--out\":\n        args.out = argv[++i];\n        break;\n      case \"--json\":\n        args.json = true;\n        break;\n      case \"--headed\":\n        args.headed = true;\n        break;\n      default:\n        return { ok: false, error: `Argumento desconhecido: ${a}` };\n    }\n  }\n  return { ok: true, args };\n}\n\nfunction printHelp(): void {\n  const available = Object.keys(FLOWS).join(\", \");\n  process.stdout.write(\n    `\\ne2e-user-zero \u2014 End-to-End QA framework as Usu\u00e1rio Zero\\n\\n` +\n      `Usage:\\n` +\n      `  bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts [options]\\n\\n` +\n      `Options:\\n` +\n      `  --mode <dogfood|focused|regression>  Execution mode (default: focused)\\n` +\n      `  --flow <name>                        Flow name (required if mode=focused)\\n` +\n      `  --baseline <run_id>                  Baseline run ID (required if mode=regression)\\n` +\n      `  --state <path>                       Auth state JSON path\\n` +\n      `  --out <dir>                          Output directory override\\n` +\n      `  --json                               Emit JSON summary to stdout only\\n` +\n      `  --headed                             Run with visible browser (debug)\\n` +\n      `  --help, -h                           Show this help\\n\\n` +\n      `Exit codes:\\n` +\n      `  0  Zero P0/P1 findings (GO)\\n` +\n      `  1  P0 or P1 findings present (NO-GO)\\n` +\n      `  2  Infra failure (browser / fs / network)\\n` +\n      `  3  Auth state expired or missing\\n` +\n      `  4  Invalid arguments\\n\\n` +\n      `Examples:\\n` +\n      `  # Focused run of one flow (Phase 5c trigger):\\n` +\n      `  bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts --mode focused --flow auth-login\\n\\n` +\n      `  # Headed debug run:\\n` +\n      `  bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts --mode focused --flow auth-login --headed\\n\\n` +\n      `  # Programmatic consumer (JSON summary only):\\n` +\n      `  bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts --mode focused --flow auth-login --json\\n\\n` +\n      `Available flows: ${available}\\n`,\n  );\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Run ID generation \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nexport function generateRunId(now: Date = new Date()): string {\n  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, \"0\");\n  const y = now.getUTCFullYear();\n  const mo = pad(now.getUTCMonth() + 1);\n  const d = pad(now.getUTCDate());\n  const h = pad(now.getUTCHours());\n  const mi = pad(now.getUTCMinutes());\n  const slug = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);\n  return `${y}${mo}${d}-${h}${mi}-${slug}`;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Output dir lifecycle (FR-E2E-018) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction ensureGitignoreCovers(repoRoot: string, pattern: string): void {\n  const giPath = path.join(repoRoot, \".gitignore\");\n  let content = \"\";\n  if (existsSync(giPath)) {\n    content = readFileSync(giPath, \"utf8\");\n  }\n  const present = content\n    .split(\"\\n\")\n    .some((line) => line.trim() === pattern);\n  if (present) return;\n  const prefix = content === \"\" || content.endsWith(\"\\n\") ? \"\" : \"\\n\";\n  appendFileSync(giPath, `${prefix}${pattern}\\n`);\n}\n\nfunction createOutputDir(\n  repoRoot: string,\n  runId: string,\n  override?: string,\n): string {\n  const outDir = override\n    ? path.resolve(override)\n    : path.join(repoRoot, \"e2e-output\", `run-${runId}`);\n  mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n  mkdirSync(path.join(outDir, \"screenshots\"), { recursive: true });\n  return outDir;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Main \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nasync function mainCli(): Promise<void> {\n  const parsed = parseArgs(process.argv);\n  if (!parsed.ok) {\n    process.stderr.write(`Erro: ${parsed.error}\\n\\nRode --help para uso.\\n`);\n    process.exit(4);\n  }\n  const args = parsed.args;\n  if (args.help) {\n    printHelp();\n    process.exit(0);\n  }\n  if (args.mode !== \"focused\") {\n    process.stderr.write(\n      `Erro: mode '${args.mode}' ainda n\u00e3o implementado em MVP \u2014 use --mode focused.\\n`,\n    );\n    process.exit(4);\n  }\n  if (!args.flow) {\n    process.stderr.write(`Erro: --mode focused requer --flow <name>.\\n`);\n    process.exit(4);\n  }\n  const flowFn = FLOWS[args.flow];\n  if (!flowFn) {\n    const available = Object.keys(FLOWS).join(\", \");\n    process.stderr.write(\n      `Erro: flow '${args.flow}' n\u00e3o encontrado. Dispon\u00edveis: ${available}\\n`,\n    );\n    process.exit(4);\n  }\n\n  const runId = generateRunId();\n  const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n\n  let outputDir: string;\n  try {\n    outputDir = createOutputDir(REPO_ROOT, runId, args.out);\n    ensureGitignoreCovers(REPO_ROOT, \"e2e-output/\");\n  } catch (e) {\n    process.stderr.write(\n      `Erro de infra: falha ao criar output dir: ${(e as Error).message}\\n`,\n    );\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n\n  const ctx: FlowContext = {\n    runId,\n    outputDir,\n    authStatePath: args.state,\n    mode: args.mode,\n  };\n\n  const driver = new BrowserDriverImpl({\n    sessionName: `e2e-${runId}-${args.flow}`,\n    authStatePath: args.state,\n    commandsLogPath: path.join(outputDir, \"commands.jsonl\"),\n    headless: !args.headed,\n  });\n\n  let flowResult: FlowResult;\n  try {\n    flowResult = flowFn(driver, ctx);\n  } catch (e) {\n    driver.close();\n    if (e instanceof BrowserDriverError) {\n      process.stderr.write(`Falha do driver: ${e.message}\\n`);\n      process.exit(2);\n    }\n    process.stderr.write(`Falha inesperada: ${(e as Error).message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(2);\n  }\n\n  driver.close();\n\n  const findings: Finding[] = classify({\n    flow: args.flow,\n    consoleMessages: flowResult.consoleMessages,\n    networkRequests: flowResult.networkRequests,\n    screenshots: flowResult.screenshots,\n  });\n\n  const endedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n  const report = buildReport({\n    runId,\n    mode: args.mode,\n    flows: [args.flow],\n    startedAt,\n    endedAt,\n    findings,\n  });\n\n  const reportMd = renderMarkdown(report);\n  writeFileSync(path.join(outputDir, \"report.md\"), reportMd);\n  writeFileSync(\n    path.join(outputDir, \"findings.json\"),\n    JSON.stringify(report, null, 2),\n  );\n\n  if (args.json) {\n    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(report.summary)}\\n`);\n  } else {\n    process.stdout.write(`${reportMd}\\n`);\n    process.stdout.write(\n      `\\n\ud83d\udcc4 Report:    ${path.relative(REPO_ROOT, path.join(outputDir, \"report.md\"))}\\n`,\n    );\n    process.stdout.write(\n      `\ud83d\udcc4 Findings:  ${path.relative(REPO_ROOT, path.join(outputDir, \"findings.json\"))}\\n`,\n    );\n    process.stdout.write(`\ud83c\udfaf Run ID:    ${runId}\\n`);\n  }\n\n  const noGo =\n    report.summary.p0_count > 0 || report.summary.p1_count > 0;\n  process.exit(noGo ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Only run main when invoked directly (not when imported by selftest).\nconst invokedDirectly =\n  typeof process.argv[1] === \"string\" && process.argv[1].includes(\"runner\");\nif (invokedDirectly) {\n  void mainCli();\n}\n"
}

The capture group is a\n// run_id slug ([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+) without the `run-` directory prefix.\nconst E2E_USER_ZERO_RE =\n  /(?:^|\\n)\\s*(?:[-*]\\s+)?(?:\\*\\*)?e2e_user_zero_last_pass(?:\\*\\*)?\\s*:\\s*`?([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)`?/gi;"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/qa/verifiers.ts",
  "old_string": "export function verifyOroCompliance(claim: ProofClaim): VerificationResult {",
  "new_string": "const E2E_RUN_ID_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/;\nconst E2E_GO_BADGE = \"\u2705 GO\";\nconst E2E_MAX_AGE_HOURS = 168; // 7 days \u00d7 24 h\n\ninterface E2EFindingsShape {\n  summary?: { p0_count?: number; p1_count?: number };\n  run_started_at?: string;\n}\n\nexport function verifyE2EUserZero(\n  claim: ProofClaim,\n  deps: VerifierDeps,\n): VerificationResult {\n  const runId = claim.expected.trim();\n  if (!E2E_RUN_ID_RE.test(runId)) {\n    return result(claim, \"skip\", runId, \"not a valid e2e run_id shape\");\n  }\n  const runDir = `e2e-output/run-${runId}`;\n  const reportPath = `${runDir}/report.md`;\n  const findingsPath = `${runDir}/findings.json`;\n\n  // Gate 1 \u2014 report.md exists on disk\n  if (!deps.fileExists(reportPath)) {\n    return result(claim, \"fail\", \"absent\", `report.md not found: ${reportPath}`);\n  }\n\n  // Gate 2 \u2014 report shows GO badge\n  const reportText = deps.readFile(reportPath);\n  if (reportText === null) {\n    return result(claim, \"skip\", \"unreadable\", `could not read ${reportPath}`);\n  }\n  if (!reportText.includes(E2E_GO_BADGE)) {\n    return result(claim, \"fail\", \"no GO badge\", \"report does not show \u2705 GO verdict\");\n  }\n\n  // Gate 3 \u2014 findings.json parses and shows zero P0/P1\n  const findingsText = deps.readFile(findingsPath);\n  if (findingsText === null) {\n    return result(claim, \"fail\", \"absent\", `findings.json not found: ${findingsPath}`);\n  }\n  let parsed: E2EFindingsShape;\n  try {\n    parsed = JSON.parse(findingsText) as E2EFindingsShape;\n  } catch {\n    return result(claim, \"fail\", \"unparseable\", \"findings.json is invalid JSON\");\n  }\n  const p0 = parsed.summary?.p0_count ?? 0;\n  const p1 = parsed.summary?.p1_count ?? 0;\n  if (p0 > 0 || p1 > 0) {\n    return result(claim, \"fail\", `P0=${p0} P1=${p1}`, \"blocking findings present\");\n  }\n\n  // Gate 4 \u2014 run started within last 7 days\n  if (parsed.run_started_at) {\n    const startedMs = Date.parse(parsed.run_started_at);\n    if (Number.isFinite(startedMs)) {\n      const ageHours = (Date.now() - startedMs) / 36e5;\n      if (ageHours > E2E_MAX_AGE_HOURS) {\n        const ageDays = Math.floor(ageHours / 24);\n        return result(\n          claim,\n          \"fail\",\n          `${ageDays}d stale`,\n          `run is older than 7 days (started ${parsed.run_started_at})`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return result(claim, \"pass\", `run-${runId}`, \"report \u2705 GO + zero P0/P1 + recent\");\n}\n\nexport function verifyOroCompliance(claim: ProofClaim): VerificationResult {"
}
```

  "new_string": "import {\n  isValidCommitHash,\n  isValidUuid,\n  parseClaimedTestCount,\n  parseActualTestCount,\n  classifyEdgeStatus,\n  verifyGitCommit,\n  verifyMeshRow,\n  verifyTsc,\n  verifyTestSuite,\n  verifyEdgeDeploy,\n  verifyFileExists,\n  verifyE2EUserZero,\n  verifyClaim,\n} from \"../../scripts/qa/verifiers\";\nimport type { ProofClaim, VerifierDeps } from \"../../scripts/qa/types\";\n\nfunction claim(kind: ProofClaim[\"kind\"], expected: string, extra: Partial<ProofClaim> = {}): ProofClaim {\n  return { kind, label: `${kind} ${expected}`, expected, raw: expected, ...extra };\n}\n\nfunction fakeDeps(overrides: Partial<VerifierDeps> = {}): VerifierDeps {\n  return {\n    gitObjectExists: () => true,\n    runTsc: () => ({ ok: true, output: \"\" }),\n    runTests: () => ({ ok: true, output: \"Test Files  3 passed (3)\\n  Tests  9 passed (9)\" }),\n    meshRowExists: async () => true,\n    edgeFunctionStatus: async () => 200,\n    fileExists: () => true,\n    readFile: () => null,\n    ...overrides,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Build a deps stub for verifyE2EUserZero with a virtual e2e-output/run-<id> dir. */\nfunction e2eDeps(opts: {\n  reportMd?: string | null;\n  findingsJson?: string | null;\n}): VerifierDeps {\n  const reportPresent = opts.reportMd !== undefined && opts.reportMd !== null;\n  const findingsPresent = opts.findingsJson !== undefined && opts.findingsJson !== null;\n  return fakeDeps({\n    fileExists: (relPath: string) => {\n      if (relPath.endsWith(\"/report.md\")) return reportPresent;\n      if (relPath.endsWith(\"/findings.json\")) return findingsPresent;\n      return true;\n    },\n    readFile: (relPath: string) => {\n      if (relPath.endsWith(\"/report.md\")) return reportPresent ? opts.reportMd ?? \"\" : null;\n      if (relPath.endsWith(\"/findings.json\")) return findingsPresent ? opts.findingsJson ?? \"\" : null;\n      return null;\n    },\n  });\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// e2e-user-zero claim: emitted by /handoff when UI-touching changes ship.\n// Matches both inline (`e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <id>`) and bullet+bold\n// (`**e2e_user_zero_last_pass:** \\`<id>\\``) forms. The capture group is a\n// run_id slug ([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+) without the `run-` directory prefix.\nconst E2E_USER_ZERO_RE =\n  /(?:^|\\n)\\s*(?:[-*]\\s+)?(?:\\*\\*)?e2e_user_zero_last_pass(?:\\*\\*)?\\s*:\\s*`?([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)`?/gi;",
  "new_string": "// e2e-user-zero claim: emitted by /handoff when UI-touching changes ship.\n// Matches both inline (`e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <id>`) and bullet+bold\n// (`- **e2e_user_zero_last_pass:** \\`<id>\\``) forms. `\\*{0,2}` is permitted at\n// every position where the markdown `**` boundary could sit \u2014 before the\n// text, between text and colon (when colon is outside the bold), and after\n// the colon (when colon is inside the bold). The capture group is a run_id\n// slug ([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+) without the `run-` directory prefix.\nconst E2E_USER_ZERO_RE =\n  /(?:^|\\n)\\s*(?:[-*]\\s+)?\\*{0,2}e2e_user_zero_last_pass\\*{0,2}:\\*{0,2}\\s*`?([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)`?/gi;"
}

## PHASE 1 — SECURITY AUDIT

Run these checks and report findings. **Block the seal if any CRITICAL finding exists.**

> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).
> A second mandatory scan runs in Phase 5b, after HANDOFF.md is written and before it is committed.

```bash
# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20

# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"

# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
```

**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.

### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)

**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**

- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`

> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.

Insert a milestone node into mcorch_nodes to mark this session in the knowledge graph:

```bash
source .env
SUPABASE_URL="https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co"
SESSION_PHASE="<phase-name-slug>"
SESSION_SUMMARY="<one-sentence summary of what was accomplished>"

curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
  -d "{
    \"name\": \"session-handoff-$(date +%Y%m%d)-${SESSION_PHASE}\",
    \"node_type\": \"handoff\",
    \"content\": \"[HANDOFF SEAL] ${SESSION_SUMMARY}\",
    \"stability_score\": 1.0,
    \"project_id\": null,
    \"user_id\": null,
    \"metadata\": {

```

```bash

  || echo "⚠️ Embedding failed — node persisted but not vectorized"

   | Commit | Conteúdo |
   |--------|----------|
   | `<hash>` | <message> |
   ...

   ### Arquitetura <Phase Name>
   ```code block with data flow or key architecture diagram```
   ```

3. **Pending Actions** — check off completed items (~~strikethrough~~), add new ones discovered this session.

4. **GraphRAG State** section — update node/edge counts.

5. **Infrastructure** table — update container health.

## PHASE 5b — HANDOFF.md SECRET SCAN (mandatory before commit)

**Run this scan on the HANDOFF.md content about to be committed. Block if any finding exists.**

```bash
# 5b-1. Scan HANDOFF.md for real credential values
grep -nE "(AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{40,}|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[a-zA-Z0-9]{32,}=\s*['\"]?[A-Za-z0-9+/]{40,})" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_\|digest\|sha256\|hash\|fingerprint" \
  | head -20 || echo "HANDOFF.md: NO CREDENTIALS FOUND ✅"

# 5b-2. Specifically check for Google API keys (AIza prefix = 39 chars total)
grep -n "AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]\{35\}" HANDOFF.md | head -10 || echo "NO GOOGLE API KEYS ✅"

# 5b-3. Check for any = "value" pattern that looks like a real assignment
grep -nE "=\s*['\"][A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20,}['\"]" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-\|YOUR_\|digest\|hash\|uuid\|id.*[0-9a-f-]\{36\}" \
  | head -10 || echo "NO INLINE ASSIGNMENTS ✅"
```

**If any check returns a match:**
1. 🔴 **DO NOT COMMIT** HANDOFF.md
2. Rewrite the offending section — replace the actual value with its location description
3. Re-run Phase 5b until all checks pass
4. Only then proceed to commit HANDOFF.md

Commit HANDOFF.md only after Phase 5b passes:
```
docs(handoff): seal <phase name> — <one-line summary>
```

---

## PHASE 5c — MATERIAL PROOF AUDIT (mandatory before push)

**Independently re-verify every material-proof claim of this seal. Block if any is contradicted.**

> ⚠️ Closes the Survival Law 1 (Materiality) self-grading weakness — this audit is mechanical,
> not self-reported. Skill: `mcorch-qa-healing` · SOP: `docs/processes/handoff-material-proof-audit.md`.

```bash
# Runs after Phase 5b, so the newest HANDOFF.md Record block is this seal's.
bun run scripts/qa/run-audit.ts HANDOFF.md
```

The audit parses the newest `## … Record` block and verifies each claim — commit hashes
(`git cat-file`), mesh UUIDs (`mcorch_nodes`/`mcorch_edges`), `tsc --noEmit`, the test suite,
edge-function reachability, file paths — then prints a Proof Manifest table and exits.

**Gate verdict:**
- **exit 0 — `SEAL ALLOWED`** → embed the Proof Manifest in the Phase 8 report (`📋 PROVA MATERIAL`); proceed to Phase 6.
- **exit 1 — `SEAL BLOCKED`** → 🔴 **BLOCK the seal.** Do NOT push. For each 🔴, fix the real
  cause — never fabricate proof (Law 1) — and re-run this phase until `SEAL ALLOWED`. If a 🔴 is a
  strict handoff-node claim, re-attempt the Phase 3 insert once (SOP Cenário D), then re-run.
- `⏭ skip` rows (infra unreachable · non-mesh UUID · size-only deploy) never block — report them.

---

## PHASE 6 — BoK SEAL STATUS (mandatory before push)

**Verify any significant module work in this session has a sealed BoK suite per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.**

```bash
# 6-1. List BoK suites present + check completeness
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] \
    && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete (9 docs)" \
    || echo "⚠️ $slug — incomplete (missing: ${missing[*]})"
done

# 6-2. Check session touched code in new src/pages/ or new supabase/functions/ that lacks BoK
session_new_modules=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~10..HEAD 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^(src/pages/|supabase/functions/|src/components/[A-Z])" | awk -F/ '{print "/"}' | sort -u)
echo "Session touched modules: $session_new_modules"

# 6-3. Mesh seal nodes for sealed BoK suites
source .env 2>/dev/null
if [ -n "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" ]; then
  curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?node_type=eq.documentation_suite&select=id,name,stability_score,created_at" \
    -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); [print(f\"  🌐 {n['name']} ({n['stability_score']})\") for n in d]" 2>/dev/null \
    || echo "  ⚠️ Could not query seal nodes"
fi
```

**Gate verdict:**
- ✅ All session modules have sealed BoK → proceed to Phase 7 push.
- ⚠️ Module work without BoK (e.g. `feat:` commit creating new page/edge fn without `docs/bok/<slug>/`)
  → 🔴 **BLOCK seal**. Run `/bok-scribe <module>` first OR add module to exemption list (typo/dep bump/single-file patch).

Report BoK status in final seal output (Phase 8).

git push origin main
```

If push fails (non-fast-forward): run `git pull --rebase origin main` first, then push again. Never force-push main.

**Invariants — never skip:**
- Security audit must run and report before any commit (Phase 1)
- **Phase 3 (milestone node) must run BEFORE Phase 4 (README badge query)** — ensures count includes the handoff node
- **Phase 5b secret scan must run on HANDOFF.md before its commit** — no exceptions
- **Phase 5c material proof audit must run and return `SEAL ALLOWED` before push** — a contradicted claim blocks the seal
- **Documentation must NEVER contain actual credential values** — only describe where they live
- README.md badge counts must reflect real DB state (query live, after milestone insert)
- HANDOFF.md must be updated even if nothing changed (session note)
- Push must happen — remote must always mirror local main
- Knowledge Mesh milestone must be attempted (failures are logged, not blocking)
- git pre-commit hook (`.git/hooks/pre-commit`) is the last barrier — do not bypass with `--no-verify`

> 41	# ✨ Constellation Orchestra
> 42	
> 43	> **Enterprise-grade Autonomous Digital Business Intelligence Platform**
> 44	> Orquestração omnichannel de marketing com IA soberana, AIOS conversacional, flywheel de receita ML fechado end-to-end, GraphRAG memory mesh, Constellation Matrix WebGL e self-healing integrado.

> 73	## 🎯 Overview
> 74	
> 75	**Constellation Orchestra** is a SaaS platform that transforms simple topics into fully monetized, multi-platform marketing campaigns. It acts as a **Harness** — a sovereign control mesh that orchestrates AI content generation, affiliate monetization (Mercado Livre), social media publishing, SEO optimization, real-time ROI tracking, and a conversational AIOS (Autonomous Intelligence Operating System).
> 76	
> 77	### Core Value Proposition
> 78	
> 79	```
> 80	1 Topic → 1 SEO Article (WordPress) + 1 B2B Post (LinkedIn) + 1 Thread (X/Twitter)
> 81	         + Affiliate Links (Mercado Livre) with per-campaign UTM attribution
> 82	         + handle-ml-postback → revenue_cents updated on every ML conversion
> 83	         + ROI Dashboard with real BRL revenue
> 84	         + GraphRAG Knowledge Node (observation inserted per run)
> 85	         + Neural HUD telemetry
> 86	         + Constellation Matrix WebGL (8597 nodes · 11930 edges — paginação completa)
> 87	         + Visual Orchestration Canvas with Quality Gates
> 88	         + AIOS — conversational AI consciousness with VAD + Barge-in
> 89	```
> 90	
> 91	### [v6.8.4] — e2e-user-zero BoK 9/9 + agent-browser SOP + canonical seed pattern (2026-05-26)
> 92	- **9ª BoK suite do projeto selada**: `docs/bok/e2e-user-zero/` 9/9 docs (2761 LOC) — framework de QA E2E que testa MCORCH como **Usuário Zero real** de interface (não API tests programáticos). Usa `agent-browser` CLI v0.27.0+ como primitiva técnica, observa 4 superfícies simultaneamente (UI/DOM accessibility-tree, Network HAR, Console messages, screenshot/video). Cada finding gera `observation` node em `mcorch_nodes` com edge `OBSERVES` para o módulo afetado.
> 93	- **Phase Commercial Gate enabler**: este BoK + primeiro dogfood pass verde são pré-requisito declarado para anúncio de Usuário 1+. Motivado pelo incident PGRST203 v6.8.2 (10 dias de regressão silenciosa no flywheel OE-661 — 2026-05-16 a 2026-05-25 — que ninguém detectou porque ninguém executou a aplicação como usuário real).
> 94	- **SOP operacional agent-browser**: `docs/processes/agent-browser-install.md` (168 LOC) — Operator / Sequence / 7 Verification Gates / 7 Recovery Paths / Success Signal. Lei 2 (Anticipated Process) anchor. CLI v0.27.0 confirmada via smoke test material (PNG 116 KB de `login.mcorch.com` hidratado em 1280×720).
> 95	- **Regra perene canonical para Phase 7 BoK seal**: `.claude/agents/bok-scribe-agent.md` Phase 7 atualizado — pattern obrigatório `scripts/seed-<slug>-bok-seal.ts` Bun-native + service role + INSERT RETURNING UUID + DERIVES_FROM edge ao parent seal. Melhoria sobre precedent (openclaw/gamificacao-nodes): o edge é inserido pelo próprio script (não apenas metadata reference) — lineage agora é queryable.
> 96	- **Schema drift fix material**: descoberta em auditoria pré-seal que docs do BoK usavam `source/target/relationship` (incorreto). Survey de produção (2026-05-26) confirmou schema canônico `source_id/target_id/relation_type`. Relation types canônicos: `defines` (678), `calls` (286), `DERIVES_FROM` (28, uppercase), `references` (4), `supersedes` (2). Corrigido em 04-frd, 05-sdd, 06-data-model, 02-brd antes do seal.
> 97	- **Material proofs**: documentation_suite UUID `5271964b-1736-4a0b-9a6b-072cb8be3697` · DERIVES_FROM edge UUID `4254225d-a666-4363-a4bd-98947efc434f` → parent mcorch-qa-healing `b00e938a-717b-4f62-b936-f4934845ec52` · handoff milestone `8e6462a9-33ed-49f1-9403-780846d1f41f` embedded 768d (`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, chunks=1) · TS zero erros · 177/177 testes em 4.53s · mesh **8603 nodes** (+3 desde v6.8.3) · 5 containers Docker healthy.
> 98	
> 99	### [v6.8.3] — mcorch-qa-healing COMMIT_RE Pure-Decimal Tightening (2026-05-25)
> 100	- **Fix definitivo do false-positive Phase 5c**: o auditor `mcorch-qa-healing` matchava qualquer `[0-9a-f]{9,40}` entre crases, incluindo timestamps de migration puramente decimais (e.g. `` `20260525173500` ``). A v6.8.2 contornou com `87961ab` reescrevendo o HANDOFF.md; v6.8.3 fecha o bug na raiz adicionando `if (/^\d+$/.test(hash)) continue;` no loop `matchAll(COMMIT_RE)`. Mesma classe de input-validation tightening da FMEA-002 (8-hex UUID-prefix exclusion).
> ```

  "new_string": "## e2e-user-zero Framework MVP Record (2026-05-26)\n\n**E2E USER ZERO FRAMEWORK MVP v6.8.5 SHIPPED (2026-05-26).** Materializa em c\u00f3digo o framework de QA E2E selado em v6.8.4 (BoK 9/9 + SOP agent-browser). **Phase Commercial Gate enabler agora operacional** \u2014 agente pode executar `bun run scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/runner.ts --mode focused --flow auth-login` e gerar `findings.json` + `report.md` com badge `\u2705 GO`/`\ud83d\udd34 NO-GO` que o gate Phase 5c (`verifyE2EUserZero`) verifica mecanicamente. A sess\u00e3o \u00e9 PURE EXECUTION da spec v6.8.4 \u2014 zero inven\u00e7\u00e3o fora dos docs can\u00f4nicos (FRD/SDD/PRD).\n\n**ORO triplet desta task (Lei 4):** Operator = MCORCH Master Execution Agent (eu); Reviewer = Sovereign (Gabriel Zarattini); Owner = Sovereign \u2014 carrega Phase Commercial Gate enabler + risco de silent-regression-prevention (alvo MTTD \u2264 24h p\u00f3s-merge).\n\n**1. Framework de 977 LOC em 7 arquivos novos** sob `scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/`. CLI runner (`runner.ts`, 296 LOC) parseia argv + dispatch para flow + lifecycle de output dir + exit codes 0/1/2/3/4 conforme FRD \u00a74.1; auto-cria `e2e-output/` e adiciona ao `.gitignore` (FR-E2E-018). Driver (`lib/browser-driver.ts`, 228 LOC) wrap-a `agent-browser` CLI v0.27.0+ via `spawnSync` s\u00edncrono \u2014 interface diverge async do FRD para MVP simplicidade (decis\u00e3o consciente documentada no commit `6239328`). Scrub de args sens\u00edveis (auth-state path, Bearer tokens, prefixes `sk_`/`eyJ`/`sbp_`/`whsec_`) antes do `commands.jsonl` write \u2014 FR-E2E-020. Classifier (`lib/classifier.ts`, 128 LOC) roda heur\u00edsticas determin\u00edsticas: HTTP 500 \u2192 P0/bug, HTTP 401 \u2192 P0/security, HTTP 404/4xx \u2192 P1/bug, `console.error` \u2192 P1/bug, `console.warn` \u2192 P3/bug. Severities encapsuladas em `classifyHttpStatus()` / `classifyConsoleType()` (exportados \u2014 test\u00e1veis isolados). Report renderer (`lib/report-renderer.ts`, 135 LOC) produz `FindingsReport` com `schema_version: \"1.0.0\"` literal + markdown com badge `\u2705 GO` (zero P0/P1) ou `\ud83d\udd34 NO-GO` \u2014 esses literais s\u00e3o o contrato do `verifyE2EUserZero()`, mudan\u00e7a quebra o gate. Finding schema (`lib/finding-schema.ts`, 115 LOC) tipa o dom\u00ednio sem imports. Flow `auth-login` (`flows/auth-login.ts`, 75 LOC) navega `https://login.mcorch.com/auth` + `wait networkidle` + screenshot inicial/final + drain console \u2014 fluxo m\u00ednimo que prova o esqueleto end-to-end.\n\n**2. Phase 5c gate ESTENDIDO (+112 LOC cir\u00fargicas).** `scripts/qa/types.ts`: `'e2e_user_zero'` adicionado ao `ClaimKind` union + `readFile(relPath): string | null` no `VerifierDeps` interface. `scripts/qa/extract-manifest.ts`: novo regex `E2E_USER_ZERO_RE` permite `\\*{0,2}` em todos os pontos onde o boundary markdown `**` pode aparecer \u2014 cobre 3 formas reais (inline, label outside bold, colon inside bold); claim emitido com `strict: true` (n\u00e3o-resolve bloqueia seal). `scripts/qa/verifiers.ts`: `verifyE2EUserZero()` implementa 4 gates sequenciais \u2014 (i) `report.md` exists; (ii) report cont\u00e9m literal `\u2705 GO`; (iii) `findings.json` parses + `summary.p0_count == 0` + `summary.p1_count == 0`; (iv) `run_started_at` est\u00e1 nas \u00faltimas 168h. Dispatch case adicionado em `verifyClaim()`. `scripts/qa/run-audit.ts`: implementa\u00e7\u00e3o real do `readFile` via `fs.readFileSync` com try/catch retornando null em qualquer falha.\n\n**3. Test coverage 177 \u2192 187** (+10 Vitest cases em 4.36s). `src/test/qa-verifiers.test.ts` ganha `describe(\"verifyE2EUserZero\", ...)` com 7 casos cobrindo pass + 6 modos de falha + 1 dispatch case. `src/test/qa-extract-manifest.test.ts` ganha 2 casos pra `E2E_USER_ZERO_RE` (inline + bullet+bold). Helper `e2eDeps()` monta `fileExists`/`readFile` sint\u00e9ticos por path-suffix sem tocar I/O real \u2014 mant\u00e9m pureza necess\u00e1ria pra rodar sob `tsconfig.app.json`.\n\n**4. Escopo MVP justificado por Lei 3 (Pruning).** SDD v6.8.4 estima 1820 LOC totais. Sess\u00e3o atual entrega esqueleto completo + 1 flow + Phase 5c integration = 977 LOC novas. Os 6 flows restantes + redactor LGPD + mesh-persistor + telemetry + selftest ficam para itera\u00e7\u00e3o seguinte (item 0c em Pending Actions) \u2014 n\u00e3o bloqueiam o gate (claim `e2e_user_zero_last_pass` \u00e9 opcional no manifest at\u00e9 que algum flow real seja executado em CI).\n\n**5. Items 2 e 3 do plano original deferidos por decis\u00e3o Sovereign refor\u00e7ada.** Antes de selar, agente apresentou status material via `AskUserQuestion` com 4 op\u00e7\u00f5es (selar / Item 2 / Item 3 / pausar). Sovereign escolheu \"Selar via /handoff agora (Recomendado)\" \u2014 Items 2 (Canvas Studio Phase 4.2 paid smoke) e 3 (Canvas Design module) confirmados como sess\u00f5es dedicadas. Ambos beneficiam-se do framework rec\u00e9m-nascido \u2014 Canvas 4.2 ser\u00e1 candidato natural para PRIMEIRA execu\u00e7\u00e3o real do flow `auth-login` (Phase 5c claim `e2e_user_zero_last_pass: <id>` emitido pela primeira vez).\n\n| Action | Result |\n|--------|--------|\n| `scripts/qa/e2e-user-zero/` 7 files (977 LOC + JSON config) | \u2705 runner + driver + classifier + report-renderer + schema + auth-login + e2e-config |\n| Phase 5c gate extended (types/extract-manifest/verifiers/run-audit) | \u2705 +112 LOC cir\u00fargicas, ClaimKind `e2e_user_zero` + `verifyE2EUserZero` 4-gate |\n|
